Report781/ 174
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 173
English
Isnad

Sufyan informed us, from al-Zuhri, from Ibn al-Musayyib, from Abu Hurayra ﵁, that the Messenger of God ﷺ said:

Matn

“A man shall not sell over the sale of his brother.” Sufyan informed us, from Ayyub, from Ibn Sirin, from Abu Hurayra, from the Prophet ﷺ, the same.

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا سُفْيَانُ، عَنِ الزُّهْرِيِّ، عَنِ ابْنِ الْمُسَيِّبِ، عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ رضي الله عنه، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ:

Matn

«لَا يَبِيعُ الرَّجُلُ عَلَى بَيْعِ أَخِيهِ» أَخْبَرَنَا سُفْيَانُ، عَنْ أَيُّوبَ، عَنِ ابْنِ سِيرِينَ، عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ، عَنِ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم مِثْلَهُ

Report783/ 174
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 173
English
Isnad

Sufyan informed us, from Abu al-Zubayr, from Jabir ﵁, that the Messenger of God ﷺ said:

Matn

“A town-dweller shall not sell for a desert-dweller; leave the people, God provides for some of them from others.”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا سُفْيَانُ، عَنْ أَبِي الزُّبَيْرِ، عَنْ جَابِرٍ رضي الله عنه، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ:

Matn

«لَا يَبِعْ حَاضِرٌ لِبَادٍ، دَعُوا النَّاسَ يَرْزُقُ اللَّهُ بَعْضُهُمْ مِنْ بَعْضٍ»

  2. 02Abu al-Zubayrأَبِي الزُّبَيْرِ
  3. 03Sufyanسُفْيَانُ

Malik informed us, from Abu al-Zinad, from al-A'raj, from Abu Hurayra ﵁, that the Messenger of God ﷺ said:

Matn

“Do not go out to meet the trade caravans.”

Matn

«لَا تَلَقُّوا السِّلَعَ»

Report785/ 174
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 174
English
Isnad

Sufyan, or Malik, informed us, from Ibn Shihab, from Humayd b. 'Abd al-Rahman and from Muhammad b. al-Nu'man b. Bashir, both narrating it from al-Nu'man b. Bashir, that

Matn

his father brought him to the Messenger of God ﷺ and said: I have given this son of mine a slave I had. The Messenger of God ﷺ said: “Have you given the like of this to all your children?” He said: No. The Messenger of God ﷺ said: “Then take it back.” Abu al-'Abbas said: This was with all our companions (reported from) Malik; that is why I made it (stand) with doubt.

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا سُفْيَانُ، أَوْ مَالِكٌ عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، عَنْ حُمَيْدِ بْنِ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ، وَعَنْ مُحَمَّدِ بْنِ النُّعْمَانِ بْنِ بَشِيرٍ، يُحَدِّثَانِهِ عَنْ نُعْمَانِ بْنِ بَشِيرٍ،

Matn

أَنَّ أَبَاهُ، أَتَى بِهِ إِلَى رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم فَقَالَ: إِنِّي نَحَلْتُ ابْنِي هَذَا غُلَامًا كَانَ لِي، فَقَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم: «أَكُلَّ وَلَدِكَ نَحَلْتَ مِثْلَ هَذَا؟» فَقَالَ: لَا، فَقَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم: «فَأَرْجِعْهُ» قَالَ أَبُو الْعَبَّاسِ: وَكَانَ هَذَا عِنْدَ أَصْحَابِنَا كُلِّهِمْ مَالِكٌ، فَلِذَلِكَ جَعَلْتُهُ بِالشَّكِّ

Transmission overview

1 path

Transmission path

Earliest to latest
  1. 01al-Nu'man b. BashirEarliest transmitterنُعْمَانِ بْنِ بَشِيرٍ
  2. 02Muhammad b. al-Nu'man b. Bashir, bothمُحَمَّدِ بْنِ النُّعْمَانِ بْنِ بَشِيرٍ، يُحَدِّثَانِهِ
  3. 03Humayd b. 'Abd al-Rahmanحُمَيْدِ بْنِ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ
  4. 04Ibn Shihabابْنِ شِهَابٍ
  5. 05Sufyan, or Malikسُفْيَانُ، أَوْ مَالِكٌ

Report786/ 174
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 174
English
Isnad

Muslim b. Khalid informed us, from Ibn Jurayj, from al-Hasan b. Muslim, from Tawus, that the Prophet ﷺ said:

Matn

“It is not lawful for a donor to take back what he gave, except a father from his child.”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا مُسْلِمُ بْنُ خَالِدٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ جُرَيْجٍ، عَنِ الْحَسَنِ بْنِ مُسْلِمٍ، عَنْ طَاوُسٍ، أَنَّ النَّبِيَّ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ:

Matn

«لَا يَحِلُّ لِوَاهِبٍ أَنْ يَرْجِعَ فِيمَا وَهَبَ إِلَّا الْوَالِدَ مِنْ وَلَدِهِ»

  1. 01TawusEarliest transmitterطَاوُسٍ
  2. 02al-Hasan b. Muslimالْحَسَنِ بْنِ مُسْلِمٍ
  3. 03Ibn Jurayjابْنِ جُرَيْجٍ
  4. 04Muslim b. Khalidمُسْلِمُ بْنُ خَالِدٍ

Report787/ 174
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 174
English
Isnad

Malik informed us, from Hisham b. 'Urwa, from his father, from 'A'isha ﵂, that she said:

Matn

Barira came to me and said: I have contracted with my owners for nine awqiyas, an uqiyya each year, so help me. 'A'isha said to her: If your owners like me to count it out to them and for your wala' to be mine, I will do so. Barira went to her owners and told them that, and they refused. Then Barira came from her owners while the Messenger of God ﷺ was sitting, and said: I offered them that and they refused unless the wala' be theirs. The Messenger of God ﷺ heard that, and the Prophet ﷺ asked her, and 'A'isha informed him. The Messenger of God ﷺ said to her: “Take her and stipulate the wala' for them, for the wala' belongs to the one who frees.” So 'A'isha ﵂ did so. Then the Messenger of God ﷺ stood among the people, praised God and extolled Him, and then said: “To proceed: What is the matter with men who stipulate conditions that are not in the Book of God? Any condition that is not in the Book of God is void, even if it be a hundred conditions; God's ruling is more deserving and His condition more binding; the wala' belongs only to the one who frees.” Malik informed us, from Yahya b. Sa'id, from 'Amra, from 'A'isha, the same.

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا مَالِكٌ، عَنْ هِشَامِ بْنِ عُرْوَةَ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، عَنْ عَائِشَةَ رضي الله عنها أَنَّهَا قَالَتْ:

Matn

جَاءَتْنِي بَرِيرَةُ فَقَالَتْ: إِنِّي كَاتَبْتُ أَهْلِي عَلَى تِسْعِ أَوَاقٍ، فِي كُلِّ عَامٍ أُوقِيَّةٌ، فَأَعِينِينِي، فَقَالَتْ لَهَا عَائِشَةُ: إِنْ أَحَبَّ أَهْلُكِ أَنْ أَعُدَّهَا لَهُمْ عَدَدْتُهَا وَيَكُونَ وَلَاؤُكِ لِي فَعَلْتُ، فَذَهَبَتْ بَرِيرَةُ إِلَى أَهْلِهَا فَقَالَتْ لَهُمْ ذَلِكَ فَأَبَوْا عَلَيْهَا، فَجَاءَتْ بَرِيرَةُ مِنْ عِنْدِ أَهْلِهَا، وَرَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم جَالِسٌ، فَقَالَتْ: إِنِّي عَرَضْتُ عَلَيْهِمْ فَأَبَوْا إِلَّا أَنْ يَكُونَ الْوَلَاءُ لَهُمْ، فَسَمِعَ ذَلِكَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم، فَسَأَلَهَا النَّبِيُّ صلى الله عليه وسلم فَأَخْبَرَتْهُ عَائِشَةُ فَقَالَ لَهَا رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم: «خُذِيهَا وَاشْتَرِطِي لَهُمُ الْوَلَاءَ، فَإِنَّ الْوَلَاءَ لِمَنْ أَعْتَقَ». فَفَعَلَتْ عَائِشَةُ رضي الله عنها، ثُمَّ قَامَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم فِي النَّاسِ فَحَمِدَ اللَّهَ وَأَثْنَى عَلَيْهِ ثُمَّ قَالَ: «أَمَّا بَعْدُ، فَمَا بَالُ رِجَالٍ يَشْتَرِطُونَ شُرُوطًا لَيْسَتْ فِي كِتَابِ اللَّهِ، مَا كَانَ مِنْ شَرْطٍ لَيْسَ فِي كِتَابِ اللَّهِ فَهُوَ بَاطِلٌ وَإِنْ كَانَ مِائَةَ شَرْطٍ، قَضَاءُ اللَّهِ أَحَقُّ، وَشَرْطُهُ أَوْثَقُ، وَإِنَّمَا الْوَلَاءُ لِمَنْ أَعْتَقَ» أَخْبَرَنَا مَالِكٌ، عَنْ يَحْيَى بْنِ سَعِيدٍ، عَنْ عَمْرَةَ، عَنْ عَائِشَةَ، مِثْلَهُ

  2. 02his fatherأَبِيهِ
  3. 03Hisham b. 'Urwaهِشَامِ بْنِ عُرْوَةَ
  4. 04Malikمَالِكٌ

English
Isnad

Isma'il b. Ibrahim b. 'Ulayya informed us, from 'Abd al-'Aziz b. Suhayb, from [p. 175] Anas ﵁, that

Matn

the Prophet ﷺ sacrificed two white-grey (amlah) rams.

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا إِسْمَاعِيلُ بْنُ إِبْرَاهِيمَ بْنِ عُلَيَّةَ، عَنْ عَبْدِ الْعَزِيزِ بْنِ صُهَيْبٍ، عَنْ ⦗ص: 175⦘ أَنَسٍ رضي الله عنه،

Matn

أَنَّ النَّبِيَّ صلى الله عليه وسلم ضَحَّى بِكَبْشَيْنِ أَمْلَحَيْنِ

Report789/ 174
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 175
English
Isnad

Sufyan informed us: 'Abd al-Rahman b. Humayd informed us, from Sa'id ibn al-Musayyib, from Umm Salama ﵂, who said: The Messenger of God ﷺ said:

Matn

“When the ten (days) have begun and one of you wishes to sacrifice, let him not touch any of his hair or skin.”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا سُفْيَانُ، أَخْبَرَنَا عَبْدُ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنُ حُمَيْدٍ، عَنْ سَعِيدِ بْنِ الْمُسَيِّبِ، عَنْ أُمِّ سَلَمَةَ رضي الله عنها قَالَتْ: قَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم:

Matn

«إِذَا دَخَلَ الْعَشْرُ فَأَرَادَ أَحَدُكُمْ أَنْ يُضَحِّيَ فَلَا يَمَسَّ مِنْ شَعْرِهِ وَلَا مِنْ بَشَرِهِ شَيْئًا»

Transmission path

  1. 01Umm SalamaEarliest transmitterأُمِّ سَلَمَةَ
  2. 02Sa'id ibn al-Musayyibسَعِيدِ بْنِ الْمُسَيِّبِ
  3. 03'Abd al-Rahman b. Humaydعَبْدُ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنُ حُمَيْدٍ
  4. 04Sufyanسُفْيَانُ

Report790/ 174
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 175
English
Isnad

Muhammad b. Isma'il b. Abi Fudayk informed us, from Ibn Abi Dhi'b, from 'Imran b. Bashir b. Muharrar, from Salim Sabalan, the client of the Nasriyyin, who said:

Matn

We set out with 'A'isha, wife of the Prophet ﷺ, for Mecca, and she used to take Ubayy out with her so that he would lead her in prayer. He said: 'Abd al-Rahman b. Abi Bakr brought water for wudu', and 'A'isha, wife of the Prophet ﷺ, said: O 'Abd al-Rahman, complete the wudu'; for I heard the Messenger of God ﷺ say: “Woe to the heels from the Fire on the Day of Resurrection.”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا مُحَمَّدُ بْنُ إِسْمَاعِيلَ بْنِ أَبِي فُدَيْكٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ أَبِي ذِئْبٍ، عَنْ عِمْرَانَ بْنِ بَشِيرِ بْنِ مُحَرَّرٍ، عَنْ سَالِمٍ سَبْلَانَ، مَوْلَى النَّصْرِيِّينَ قَالَ:

Matn

خَرَجْنَا مَعَ عَائِشَةَ زَوْجِ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم إِلَى مَكَّةَ، وَكَانَتْ تَخْرُجُ بِأُبَيٍّ حَتَّى يُصَلِّيَ بِهَا، قَالَ: فَأَتَى عَبْدُ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنُ أَبِي بَكْرٍ بِوَضُوءٍ فَقَالَتْ عَائِشَةُ زَوْجُ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم: يَا عَبْدَ الرَّحْمَنِ، أَسْبِغِ الْوُضُوءَ؛ فَإِنِّي سَمِعْتُ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم يَقُولُ: «وَيْلٌ لِلْأَعْقَابِ مِنَ النَّارِ يَوْمَ الْقِيَامَةِ»

Transmission path

Earliest to latest

  2. 02'Imran b. Bashir b. Muharrarعِمْرَانَ بْنِ بَشِيرِ بْنِ مُحَرَّرٍ
  3. 03Ibn Abi Dhi'bابْنِ أَبِي ذِئْبٍ
  4. 04Muhammad b. Isma'il b. Abi Fudaykمُحَمَّدُ بْنُ إِسْمَاعِيلَ بْنِ أَبِي فُدَيْكٍ

Isnad

Sufyan informed us, from Ibn 'Ajlan, from Sa'id ibn Abi Sa'id, from 'A'isha ﵂, that

Matn

she said to 'Abd al-Rahman: Complete the wudu', O 'Abd al-Rahman; for I heard the Messenger of God ﷺ say: “Woe to the heels from the Fire.”

أَنَّهَا قَالَتْ لِعَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ: أَسْبِغِ الْوُضُوءَ يَا عَبْدَ الرَّحْمَنِ؛ فَإِنِّي سَمِعْتُ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم يَقُولُ: «وَيْلٌ لِلْأَعْقَابِ مِنَ النَّارِ»

Report792/ 174
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 175
English
Isnad

Sufyan informed us, from Ibn 'Ajlan, from 'Asim b. 'Umar b. Qatada, from Mahmud b. Labid, from Rafi' b. Khadij ﵁, that the Prophet ﷺ said:

Matn

“Pray the dawn prayer when it is bright, for that is greater for your rewards”—or he said: “for the reward.”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا سُفْيَانُ، عَنِ ابْنِ عَجْلَانَ، عَنْ عَاصِمِ بْنِ عُمَرَ بْنِ قَتَادَةَ، عَنْ مَحْمُودِ بْنِ لَبِيدٍ، عَنْ رَافِعِ بْنِ خَدِيجٍ رضي الله عنه، أَنَّ النَّبِيَّ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ:

Matn

«أَسْفِرُوا بِالصُّبْحِ؛ فَإِنَّ ذَلِكَ أَعْظَمُ لِأُجُورِكُمْ». أَوْ قَالَ: «لِلْأَجْرِ»

  1. 01Rafi' b. KhadijEarliest transmitterرَافِعِ بْنِ خَدِيجٍ
  2. 02Mahmud b. Labidمَحْمُودِ بْنِ لَبِيدٍ

  4. 04Ibn 'Ajlanابْنِ عَجْلَانَ

Report793/ 174
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 175
English
Isnad

Sufyan informed us, from al-Zuhri, from 'Urwa, from 'A'isha ﵂, who said:

Matn

“Believing women used to pray with the Prophet ﷺ, wrapped in their cloaks, and then return to their families, with no one recognizing them because of the darkness.”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا سُفْيَانُ، عَنِ الزُّهْرِيِّ، عَنْ عُرْوَةَ، عَنْ عَائِشَةَ رضي الله عنها قَالَتْ:

Matn

«كُنَّ نِسَاءٌ مِنَ الْمُؤْمِنَاتِ يُصَلِّينَ مَعَ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم وَهُنَّ مُتَلَفِّعَاتٌ بِمُرُوطِهِنَّ ثُمَّ يَرْجِعْنَ إِلَى أَهْلِهِنَّ مَا يَعْرِفُهُنَّ أَحَدٌ مِنَ الْغَلَسِ»

Report794/ 174
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 176
English
Isnad

Sufyan informed us, from al-Zuhri, from Salim, from his father ﵁, who said:

Matn

“I saw the Messenger of God ﷺ, when he began the prayer, raise his hands until they were level with his shoulders, and when he intended to bow, and after he raised his head from the bowing; and he did not raise (them) between the two prostrations.”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا سُفْيَانُ، عَنِ الزُّهْرِيِّ، عَنْ سَالِمٍ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ رضي الله عنه قَالَ:

Matn

«رَأَيْتُ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم سَلَّمَ إِذَا افْتَتَحَ الصَّلَاةَ رَفَعَ يَدَيْهِ حَتَّى يُحَاذِيَ مَنْكِبَيْهِ، وَإِذَا أَرَادَ أَنْ يَرْكَعَ، وَبَعْدَمَا يَرْفَعُ رَأْسَهُ مِنَ الرُّكُوعِ، وَلَا يَرْفَعُ بَيْنَ السَّجْدَتَيْنِ»

Report795/ 174
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 176
English
Isnad

Sufyan informed us, from 'Asim b. Kulayb, who said: I heard my father say: Wa'il b. Hujr narrated to me, saying:

Matn

“I saw the Messenger of God ﷺ, when he began the prayer, raise his hands level with his shoulders, and (also) when he bowed, and after he raised his head.” Wa'il said: “Then I came to them in the winter and saw them raising their hands inside their hooded cloaks.”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا سُفْيَانُ، عَنْ عَاصِمِ بْنِ كُلَيْبٍ قَالَ: سَمِعْتُ أَبِي يَقُولُ: حَدَّثَنِي وَائِلُ بْنُ حُجْرٍ قَالَ:

Matn

" رَأَيْتُ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم إِذَا افْتَتَحَ الصَّلَاةَ يَرْفَعُ يَدَيْهِ حَذْوَ مَنْكِبَيْهِ، وَإِذَا رَكَعَ، وَبَعْدَمَا يَرْفَعُ رَأْسَهُ. قَالَ وَائِلٌ: ثُمَّ أَتَيْتُهُمْ فِي الشِّتَاءِ فَرَأَيْتُهُمْ يَرْفَعُونَ أَيْدِيَهُمْ فِي الْبَرَانِسِ "

Report796/ 174
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 176
English
Isnad

Sufyan informed us, from Yazid b. Abi Ziyad, from 'Abd al-Rahman b. Abi Layla, from al-Bara' b. 'Azib ﵁, who said:

Matn

“I saw the Messenger of God ﷺ, when he began the prayer, raise his hands.” Sufyan said: Then I came to Kufa and met Yazid and heard him narrating it, and he added in it: “then he did not repeat (it)”; so I thought they had prompted him. Sufyan said: Thus I heard Yazid narrate it, and then I heard him afterwards narrate it thus, with Yazid adding: then he did not repeat. Al-Shafi'i ﵁ said: Sufyan tended to deem Yazid mistaken in this hadith and to say: It is as if he was prompted with this other phrase and took it up; and Sufyan did not regard Yazid as a good memorizer.

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا سُفْيَانُ، عَنْ يَزِيدَ بْنِ أَبِي زِيَادٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنِ أَبِي لَيْلَى، عَنِ الْبَرَاءِ بْنِ عَازِبٍ رضي الله عنه قَالَ:

Matn

«رَأَيْتُ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم إِذَا افْتَتَحَ الصَّلَاةَ رَفَعَ يَدَيْهِ» قَالَ سُفْيَانُ: ثُمَّ قَدِمْتُ الْكُوفَةَ فَلَقِيتُ يَزِيدَ فَسَمِعْتُهُ يُحَدِّثُ بِهَا وَزَادَ فِيهِ: «ثُمَّ لَا يَعُودُ، فَظَنَنْتُ أَنَّهُمْ لَقَّنُوهُ». قَالَ سُفْيَانُ: هَكَذَا سَمِعْتُ يَزِيدَ يُحَدِّثُهُ، ثُمَّ سَمِعْتُهُ بَعْدُ يُحَدِّثُهُ هَكَذَا وَيَزِيدُ فِيهِ: ثُمَّ لَا يَعُودُ. قَالَ الشَّافِعِيُّ رضي الله عنه: وَذَهَبَ سُفْيَانُ إِلَى أَنْ يُغَلِّطَ يَزِيدَ فِي هَذَا الْحَدِيثِ وَيَقُولُ: كَأَنَّهُ لَقِنَ هَذَا الْحَرْفَ الْآخَرَ فَتَلَقَّنَهُ، وَلَمْ يَكُنْ سُفْيَانُ يَرَى يَزِيدَ بِالْحِفْظِ كَذَلِكَ

  3. 03Yazid b. Abi Ziyadيَزِيدَ بْنِ أَبِي زِيَادٍ

Report797/ 174
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 176
English
Isnad

Sufyan b. 'Uyayna informed us, from Husayn, I think from Hilal b. Yasaf, who said:

Matn

Ziyad b. Abi al-Ja'd took me by the hand and made me stand before an old man at al-Raqqa from among the Companions of the Messenger of God ﷺ, called Wabisa b. Ma'bad, and said: This man informed me that the Messenger of God ﷺ saw a man praying alone behind the row, and ordered him to repeat the prayer.

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا سُفْيَانُ بْنُ عُيَيْنَةَ، عَنْ حُصَيْنٍ، أَظُنُّهُ عَنْ هِلَالِ بْنِ يَسَافٍ قَالَ:

Matn

أَخَذَ بِيَدِي زِيَادُ بْنُ أَبِي الْجَعْدِ فَوَقَفَ بِي عَلَى شَيْخٍ بِالرَّقَّةِ مِنْ أَصْحَابِ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم يُقَالُ لَهُ وَابِصَةُ بْنُ مَعْبَدٍ فَقَالَ: أَخْبَرَنِي هَذَا أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم رَأَى رَجُلًا يُصَلِّي خَلْفَ الصَّفِّ وَحْدَهُ فَأَمَرَهُ أَنْ يُعِيدَ الصَّلَاةَ

Report798/ 174
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 176
English
Isnad

Malik b. Abi Talha informed us, from Anas b. Malik ﵁, that

Matn

his grandmother Mulayka invited the Prophet ﷺ to a meal she had made for him; he ate of it and then said: “Rise, so that I may lead you in prayer.” [p. 177] Anas said: I went to a mat of ours that had darkened from long use and sprinkled it with water; the Messenger of God ﷺ stood, and I and the orphan formed a row behind him, and the old woman behind us; he prayed two rak'as for us and then left.

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا مَالِكُ بْنُ أَبِي طَلْحَةَ، عَنْ أَنَسِ بْنِ مَالِكٍ رضي الله عنه

Matn

أَنَّ جَدَّتَهُ، مُلَيْكَةَ دَعَتِ النَّبِيَّ صلى الله عليه وسلم إِلَى طَعَامٍ صَنَعَتْهُ لَهُ، فَأَكَلَ مِنْهُ ثُمَّ قَالَ: «قُومُوا فَلْأُصَلِّي بِكُمْ» ⦗ص: 177⦘ . قَالَ أَنَسٌ: فَقُمْتُ إِلَى حَصِيرٍ لَنَا قَدِ اسْوَدَّ مِنْ طُولِ مَا لُبِسَ فَنَضَحْتُهُ بِمَاءٍ، فَقَامَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم وَصَفَفْتُ أَنَا وَالْيَتِيمُ وَرَاءَهُ، وَالْعَجُوزُ مِنْ وَرَائِنَا، فَصَلَّى لَنَا رَكْعَتَيْنِ ثُمَّ انْصَرَفَ

  2. 02Malik b. Abi Talhaمَالِكُ بْنُ أَبِي طَلْحَةَ

Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 177
English
Isnad

Sufyan informed us, from Ishaq b. 'Abdullah b. Abi Talha, that he heard his paternal uncle, Anas b. Malik, say:

Matn

“I and an orphan of ours prayed in our house behind the Messenger of God ﷺ, with Umm Sulaym behind us.”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا سُفْيَانُ، عَنْ إِسْحَاقَ بْنِ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ أَبِي طَلْحَةَ، أَنَّهُ سَمِعَ عَمَّهُ، أَنَسَ بْنَ مَالِكٍ يَقُولُ:

Matn

«صَلَّيْتُ أَنَا وَيَتِيمٌ، لَنَا فِي بَيْتِنَا خَلْفَ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم، وَأُمُّ سُلَيْمٍ خَلْفَنَا»

Report800/ 174
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 177
English
Isnad

Malik informed us, from Yazid b. Ruman, from Salih b. Khawwat, from one who

Matn

had prayed the fear prayer with the Prophet ﷺ on the day of Dhat al-Riqa', that one group formed a row with him while another group faced the enemy; he prayed with those who were with him one rak'a, then remained standing while they completed (the prayer) for themselves, and then they left and formed a row facing the enemy; then the other group came and he prayed with them the rak'a that remained for him, then stayed sitting while they completed for themselves, and then he gave the salam with them. One who heard 'Abdullah b. 'Umar b. Hafs narrating from his brother 'Ubaydullah, from al-Qasim b. Muhammad, from Salih b. Khawwat b. Jubayr, from Khawwat b. Jubayr, from the Prophet ﷺ, informed us with a similar meaning, not contradicting it.

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا مَالِكٌ، عَنْ يَزِيدَ بْنِ رُومَانَ، عَنْ صَالِحِ بْنِ خَوَّاتٍ،

Matn

عَمَّنْ صَلَّى مَعَ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم يَوْمَ ذَاتِ الرِّقَاعِ صَلَاةَ الْخَوْفِ أَنَّ طَائِفَةً صُفَّتْ مَعَهُ وَطَائِفَةً وِجَاهَ الْعَدُوِّ، فَصَلَّى بِالَّذِينَ مَعَهُ رَكْعَةً ثُمَّ ثَبَتَ قَائِمًا وَأَتَمُّوا لِأَنْفُسِهِمْ ثُمَّ انْصَرَفُوا فَصَفُّوا وِجَاهَ الْعَدُوِّ، وَجَاءَتِ الطَّائِفَةُ الْأُخْرَى فَصَلَّى بِهِمُ الرَّكْعَةَ الَّتِي بَقِيَتْ عَلَيْهِ ثُمَّ ثَبَتَ جَالِسًا وَأَتَمُّوا لِأَنْفُسِهِمْ ثُمَّ سَلَّمَ بِهِمْ أَخْبَرَنَا مَنْ، سَمِعَ عَبْدَ اللَّهِ بْنَ عُمَرَ بْنِ حَفْصٍ، يَذْكُرُ عَنْ أَخِيهِ، عُبَيْدِ اللَّهِ، عَنِ الْقَاسِمِ بْنِ مُحَمَّدٍ، عَنْ صَالِحِ بْنِ خَوَّاتِ بْنِ جُبَيْرٍ، عَنْ خَوَّاتِ بْنِ جُبَيْرٍ، عَنِ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم مِثْلَ مَعْنَاهُ لَا يُخَالِفُهُ

Report801/ 174
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 177
English
Isnad

Malik informed us, from Zayd b. Aslam, from 'Ata' b. Yasar, from Ibn 'Abbas ﵄, who said:

Matn

The sun was eclipsed and the Messenger of God ﷺ prayed; and Ibn 'Abbas related that his prayer was two rak'as, with two returns (raj'atayn) in each rak'a. Then he addressed them, saying: “The sun and the moon are two signs among the signs of God (Mighty and Majestic); they are not eclipsed for the death of anyone nor for his life; so when you see that, hasten to the remembrance of God.”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا مَالِكٌ، عَنْ زَيْدِ بْنِ أَسْلَمَ، عَنْ عَطَاءِ بْنِ يَسَارٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ عَبَّاسٍ رضي الله عنهما قَالَ:

Matn

خَسَفَتِ الشَّمْسُ فَصَلَّى رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم، فَحَكَى ابْنُ عَبَّاسٍ أَنَّ صَلَاتَهُ رَكْعَتَانِ، فِي كُلِّ رَكْعَةٍ رَجْعَتَيْنِ، ثُمَّ خَطَبَهُمْ فَقَالَ: «إِنَّ الشَّمْسَ وَالْقَمَرَ آيَتَانِ مِنْ آيَاتِ اللَّهِ عز وجل، لَا يَخْسِفَانِ لِمَوْتِ أَحَدٍ وَلَا لِحَيَاتِهِ، فَإِذَا رَأَيْتُمْ ذَلِكَ فَافْزَعُوا إِلَى ذِكْرِ اللَّهِ»

Report802/ 174
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 177
English
Isnad

Malik informed us, from Hisham b. 'Urwa, from his father, from 'A'isha ﵂ [p. 178] and Malik informed us, from Yahya b. Sa'id, from 'Amra, from 'A'isha ﵂, who said:

Matn

“The sun was eclipsed and the Prophet ﷺ prayed”; and she related that he prayed two rak'as, with two rak'as in each rak'a.

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا مَالِكٌ، عَنْ هِشَامِ بْنِ عُرْوَةَ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، عَنْ عَائِشَةَ رضي الله عنها ⦗ص: 178⦘ وَأَخْبَرَنَا مَالِكٌ، عَنْ يَحْيَى بْنِ سَعِيدٍ، عَنْ عَمْرَةَ، عَنْ عَائِشَةَ رضي الله عنها قَالَتْ:

Matn

«خَسَفَتِ الشَّمْسُ فَصَلَّى النَّبِيُّ صلى الله عليه وسلم، فَحَكَتْ أَنَّهُ صَلَّى رَكْعَتَيْنِ، فِي كُلِّ رَكْعَةٍ رَكْعَتَيْنِ»

Report803/ 174
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 178
English
Isnad

The trustworthy one informed us, from Ma'mar, from al-Zuhri, from Kathir b. 'Abbas b. 'Abd al-Muttalib, that

Matn

the Messenger of God ﷺ prayed at the eclipse of the sun two rak'as, with two rak'as in each rak'a.

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا الثِّقَةُ، عَنْ مَعْمَرٍ، عَنِ الزُّهْرِيِّ، عَنْ كَثِيرِ بْنِ عَبَّاسِ بْنِ عَبْدِ الْمُطَّلِبِ،

Matn

أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم صَلَّى فِي كُسُوفِ الشَّمْسِ رَكْعَتَيْنِ، فِي كُلِّ رَكْعَةٍ رَكْعَتَانِ

Report804/ 174
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 178
English
Isnad

Sufyan informed us, from Isma'il b. Abi Khalid, from Qays b. Abi Hazim, from Abu Mas'ud al-Ansari, who said:

Matn

The sun was eclipsed on the day Ibrahim, the son of the Messenger of God ﷺ, died, and the people said: The sun has been eclipsed for the death of Ibrahim. The Prophet ﷺ said: “The sun and the moon are two signs among the signs of God; they are not eclipsed for the death of anyone nor for his life; so when you see that, hasten to the remembrance of God and to prayer.”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا سُفْيَانُ، عَنْ إِسْمَاعِيلَ بْنِ أَبِي خَالِدٍ، عَنْ قَيْسِ بْنِ أَبِي حَازِمٍ، عَنْ أَبِي مَسْعُودٍ الْأَنْصَارِيِّ قَالَ:

Matn

انْكَسَفَتِ الشَّمْسُ يَوْمَ مَاتَ إِبْرَاهِيمُ ابْنُ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم فَقَالَ النَّاسُ: انْكَسَفَتِ الشَّمْسُ لِمَوْتِ إِبْرَاهِيمَ، فَقَالَ النَّبِيُّ صلى الله عليه وسلم: «إِنَّ الشَّمْسَ وَالْقَمَرَ آيَتَانِ مِنْ آيَاتِ اللَّهِ، لَا يَنْكَسِفَانِ لِمَوْتِ أَحَدٍ وَلَا لِحَيَاتِهِ، فَإِذَا رَأَيْتُمْ ذَلِكَ فَافْزَعُوا إِلَى ذِكْرِ اللَّهِ وَإِلَى الصَّلَاةِ»

Transmission overview

1 path

Transmission path

Earliest to latest
  1. 01Abu Mas'ud al-AnsariEarliest transmitterأَبِي مَسْعُودٍ الْأَنْصَارِيِّ
  2. 02Qays b. Abi Hazimقَيْسِ بْنِ أَبِي حَازِمٍ
  3. 03Isma'il b. Abi Khalidإِسْمَاعِيلَ بْنِ أَبِي خَالِدٍ
  4. 04Sufyanسُفْيَانُ

Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 178
English
Isnad

Sufyan informed us, from Sulayman al-Ahwal, who said: I heard Tawus say:

Matn

“The sun was eclipsed and Ibn 'Abbas led us in the portico of Zamzam in six bowings and then four prostrations.”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا سُفْيَانُ، عَنْ سُلَيْمَانَ الْأَحْوَلِ، يَقُولُ: سَمِعْتُ طَاوُسًا، يَقُولُ:

Matn

«خَسَفَتِ الشَّمْسُ فَصَلَّى بِنَا ابْنُ عَبَّاسٍ فِي صُفَّةِ زَمْزَمَ سِتَّ رَكَعَاتٍ، ثُمَّ أَرْبَعَ سَجَدَاتٍ»

Report806/ 174
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 178
English
Isnad

Malik informed us, from 'Abdullah b. 'Abd al-Rahman b. Ma'mar al-Ansari, from Abu Yunus, the client of 'A'isha, Mother of the Believers, from 'A'isha ﵂, that

Matn

a man said to the Messenger of God ﷺ, while he was standing at the door and I was listening: O Messenger of God, I wake up in a state of major impurity while I intend to fast. The Messenger of God ﷺ said: “And I wake up in a state of major impurity while intending to fast; so I wash and fast that day.”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا مَالِكٌ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنِ مَعْمَرٍ الْأَنْصَارِيِّ، عَنْ أَبِي يُونُسَ، مَوْلَى عَائِشَةَ أُمِّ الْمُؤْمِنِينَ، عَنْ عَائِشَةَ رضي الله عنها

Matn

أَنَّ رَجُلًا، قَالَ لِرَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم وَهُوَ وَاقِفٌ عَلَى الْبَابِ وَأَنَا أَسْمَعُ: يَا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ، إِنِّي أُصْبِحُ جُنُبًا وَأَنَا أُرِيدُ الصَّوْمَ، فَقَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم: «وَأَنَا أُصْبِحُ جُنُبًا وَأَنَا أُرِيدُ الصَّوْمَ فَأَغْتَسِلُ وَأَصُومُ ذَلِكَ الْيَوْمَ»

Transmission path

Earliest to latest
  1. 01'A'ishaEarliest transmitterعَائِشَةَ
  2. 02Abu Yunus, the client of 'A'isha, Mother of the Believersأَبِي يُونُسَ، مَوْلَى عَائِشَةَ أُمِّ الْمُؤْمِنِينَ
  3. 03'Abdullah b. 'Abd al-Rahman b. Ma'mar al-Ansariعَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنِ مَعْمَرٍ الْأَنْصَارِيِّ

No. 806
Compilation
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī

From the Book of Divergent Hadith (Ikhtilāf al-Ḥadīth), excluding repeats

806

Dār al-Kutub al-ʿIlmiyya, Beirut, 1400 AH (1980), corrected against the Būlāq and Indian printings
Edition page
Vol. 1, p. 178
Source record
1363572
Permalink ↗
Report807/ 174
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 178
English
Isnad

Malik informed us, from Sumayy, the client of Abu Bakr, that he heard Abu Bakr b. 'Abd al-Rahman say:

Matn

I and my father were with Marwan b. al-Hakam, who was then the governor of Medina, and it was mentioned to him that Abu Hurayra says: Whoever wakes up in a state of major impurity has broken his fast for that day. Marwan said: I adjure you, 'Abd al-Rahman, [p. 179] to go to the two Mothers of the Believers, 'A'isha and Umm Salama, and ask them about that. Abu Bakr said: 'Abd al-Rahman went and I went with him until we entered upon 'A'isha; 'Abd al-Rahman greeted her with salam and said: O Mother of the Believers, we were with Marwan, and it was mentioned to him that Abu Hurayra said: Whoever wakes up in a state of major impurity has broken his fast for that day. 'A'isha said: It is not as Abu Hurayra said. O 'Abd al-Rahman, do you turn away from what the Messenger of God ﷺ used to do? 'Abd al-Rahman said: No, by God. 'A'isha said: Then I bear witness about the Messenger of God ﷺ that he would wake up in a state of major impurity from intercourse, not from a nocturnal emission, and then fast that day. He said: Then we went out until we entered upon Umm Salama ﵂, and he asked her about that, and she said the same as 'A'isha had said. We went out until we came to Marwan, and 'Abd al-Rahman said to him: What did the two of them say? And he informed him. Marwan said: I adjure you, Abu Muhammad, to ride my mount at the door and go to Abu Hurayra and inform him of that. So 'Abd al-Rahman rode and I rode until we came to Abu Hurayra; 'Abd al-Rahman spoke with him for a while and then mentioned that to him, and Abu Hurayra said: I have no knowledge of that; someone informed me of it.

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا مَالِكٌ، عَنْ سُمَيٍّ، مَوْلَى أَبِي بَكْرٍ أَنَّهُ سَمِعَ أَبَا بَكْرِ بْنَ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ، يَقُولُ:

Matn

كُنْتُ أَنَا وَأَبِي، عِنْدَ مَرْوَانَ بْنِ الْحَكَمِ وَهُوَ أَمِيرُ الْمَدِينَةِ، فَذُكِرَ لَهُ أَنَّ أَبَا هُرَيْرَةَ يَقُولُ: مَنْ أَصْبَحَ جُنُبًا أَفْطَرَ ذَلِكَ الْيَوْمَ، فَقَالَ مَرْوَانُ: أَقْسَمْتُ عَلَيْكَ يَا عَبْدَ الرَّحْمَنِ ⦗ص: 179⦘ لَتَذْهَبَنَّ إِلَى أُمَّيِ الْمُؤْمِنِينَ عَائِشَةَ وَأُمِّ سَلَمَةَ فَلَتَسْأَلَنَّهُمَا عَنْ ذَلِكَ، فَقَالَ أَبُو بَكْرٍ: فَذَهَبَ عَبْدُ الرَّحْمَنِ وَذَهَبْتُ مَعَهُ حَتَّى دَخَلْنَا عَلَى عَائِشَةَ فَسَلَّمَ عَلَيْهَا عَبْدُ الرَّحْمَنِ فَقَالَ: يَا أُمَّ الْمُؤْمِنِينَ، إِنَّا كُنَّا عِنْدَ مَرْوَانَ فَذُكِرَ لَهُ أَنَّ أَبَا هُرَيْرَةَ قَالَ: مَنْ أَصْبَحَ جُنُبًا أَفْطَرَ ذَلِكَ الْيَوْمَ، فَقَالَتْ عَائِشَةُ: لَيْسَ كَمَا قَالَ أَبُو هُرَيْرَةَ، يَا عَبْدَ الرَّحْمَنِ، أَتَرْغَبُ عَمَّا كَانَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم يَفْعَلُهُ؟ قَالَ عَبْدُ الرَّحْمَنِ: لَا وَاللَّهِ، فَقَالَتْ عَائِشَةُ: فَأَشْهَدُ عَلَى رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم " إِنْ كَانَ لَيُصْبِحُ جُنُبًا مِنْ جِمَاعٍ غَيْرَ احْتِلَامٍ ثُمَّ يَصُومُ ذَلِكَ الْيَوْمَ. قَالَ: ثُمَّ خَرَجْنَا حَتَّى دَخَلْنَا عَلَى أُمِّ سَلَمَةَ رَضِيَ اللَّهُ فَسَأَلَهَا عَنْ ذَلِكَ، فَقَالَتْ مِثْلَ مَا قَالَتْ عَائِشَةُ "، فَخَرَجْنَا حَتَّى جِئْنَا مَرْوَانَ فَقَالَ لَهُ عَبْدُ الرَّحْمَنِ: مَا قَالَتَا؟ فَأَخْبَرَهُ، فَقَالَ مَرْوَانُ: أَقْسَمْتُ عَلَيْكَ يَا أَبَا مُحَمَّدٍ لَتَرْكَبَنَّ دَابَّتِي بِالْبَابِ فَلَتَأْتِيَنَّ أَبَا هُرَيْرَةَ، فَلَتُخْبِرَنَّهُ بِذَلِكَ، فَرَكِبَ عَبْدُ الرَّحْمَنِ وَرَكِبْتُ حَتَّى أَتَيْنَا أَبَا هُرَيْرَةَ، فَتَحَدَّثَ مَعَهُ عَبْدُ الرَّحْمَنِ سَاعَةً ثُمَّ ذَكَرَ لَهُ ذَلِكَ فَقَالَ أَبُو هُرَيْرَةَ: لَا عِلْمَ لِي بِذَلِكَ، إِنَّمَا أَخْبَرَنِيهِ مُخْبِرٌ

English
Isnad

Sufyan informed us: Sumayy, the client of Abu Bakr, narrated to us, from Abu Bakr b. 'Abd al-Rahman b. al-Harith, from 'A'isha, that she said:

Matn

“The dawn would overtake the Prophet ﷺ while he was in a state of major impurity, and he would wash and fast his day.”

Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا سُفْيَانُ، حَدَّثَنَا سُمَيٌّ مَوْلَى أَبِي بَكْرٍ، عَنْ أَبِي بَكْرِ بْنِ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنِ الْحَارِثِ، عَنْ عَائِشَةَ، أَنَّهَا قَالَتْ:

Report809/ 174
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 179
English
Isnad

'Abd al-Wahhab informed us, from Khalid al-Hadhdha', from Abu Qilaba, from Abu al-Ash'ath, from Shaddad b. Aws ﵁, who said:

Matn

We were with the Prophet ﷺ at the time of the Conquest, and he saw a man cupping himself on the eighteenth night having passed of Ramadan, and he said, while holding my hand: “The cupper and the one cupped have broken their fast.”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا عَبْدُ الْوَهَّابِ، عَنْ خَالِدٍ الْحَذَّاءِ، عَنْ أَبِي قِلَابَةَ، عَنْ أَبِي الْأَشْعَثِ، عَنْ شَدَّادِ بْنِ أَوْسٍ رضي الله عنه قَالَ:

Matn

كُنَّا مَعَ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم زَمَانَ الْفَتْحِ فَرَأَى رَجُلًا يَحْتَجِمُ لِثَمَانَ عَشْرَةَ لَيْلَةً خَلَتْ مِنْ رَمَضَانَ فَقَالَ وَهُوَ آخِذٌ بِيَدِي: «أَفْطَرَ الْحَاجِمُ وَالْمَحْجُومُ»

  2. 02Abu al-Ash'athأَبِي الْأَشْعَثِ
  3. 03Abu Qilabaأَبِي قِلَابَةَ
  4. 04Khalid al-Hadhdha'خَالِدٍ الْحَذَّاءِ
  5. 05'Abd al-Wahhabعَبْدُ الْوَهَّابِ

English
Isnad

Sufyan informed us, from Yazid b. Abi Ziyad, from Miqsam, from Ibn 'Abbas ﵁, that

Matn

the Messenger of God ﷺ had himself cupped while in ihram and fasting.

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا سُفْيَانُ، عَنْ يَزِيدَ بْنِ أَبِي زِيَادٍ، عَنْ مِقْسَمٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ عَبَّاسٍ، رَضِيَ اللَّهُ

Matn

أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم احْتَجَمَ مُحْرِمًا صَائِمًا

English
Isnad

Sufyan informed us, from 'Amr b. Dinar, from Ibn Shihab, who said: Yazid informed me that

Matn

the Messenger of God ﷺ married while he was not in ihram. 'Amr said: I said to Ibn Shihab: Do you set Yazid b. al-Asamm against Ibn 'Abbas?

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا سُفْيَانُ، عَنْ عَمْرِو بْنِ دِينَارٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ قَالَ: أَخْبَرَنِي يَزِيدُ،

Matn

أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم نَكَحَ وَهُوَ حَلَالٌ قَالَ عَمْرٌو: فَقُلْتُ لِابْنِ شِهَابٍ: أَتَجْعَلُ يَزِيدَ بْنِ الْأَصَمِّ، ابْنَ عَبَّاسٍ؟

English
Isnad

Sufyan informed us, from Ayyub b. Musa, from Nubayh b. Wahb, from Aban b. 'Uthman, from 'Uthman ﵁, that the Messenger of God ﷺ said:

Matn

“The one in ihram does not marry, nor propose.”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا سُفْيَانُ، عَنْ أَيُّوبَ بْنِ مُوسَى، عَنْ نُبَيْهِ بْنِ وَهْبٍ، عَنْ أَبَانَ بْنِ عُثْمَانَ، عَنْ عُثْمَانَ رضي الله عنه، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ:

Matn

«الْمُحْرِمُ لَا يَنْكِحُ، وَلَا يَخْطُبُ»

Report813/ 174
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 180
English
Isnad

Malik informed us, from Nafi', from Nubayh b. Wahb, one of Banu 'Abd al-Dar, from Aban b. 'Uthman, from 'Uthman ﵁, that the Messenger of God ﷺ said:

Matn

“The one in ihram shall not marry, nor give in marriage, nor propose.”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا مَالِكٌ، عَنْ نَافِعٍ، عَنْ نُبَيْهِ بْنِ وَهْبٍ، أَحَدِ بَنِي عَبْدِ الدَّارِ، عَنْ أَبَانَ بْنِ عُثْمَانَ، عَنْ عُثْمَانَ رضي الله عنه، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ:

Matn

«لَا يَنْكِحُ الْمُحْرِمُ، وَلَا يُنْكِحُ، وَلَا يَخْطُبُ»

Report814/ 174
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 180
English
Isnad

Malik informed us, from Rabi'a b. Abi 'Abd al-Rahman, from Sulayman b. Yasar, that

Matn

the Messenger of God ﷺ sent Abu Rafi', his client, and a man of the Ansar, and they married Maymuna to him, while the Prophet ﷺ was in Medina.

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا مَالِكٌ، عَنْ رَبِيعَةَ بْنِ أَبِي عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ، عَنْ سُلَيْمَانَ بْنِ يَسَارٍ،

Matn

أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم بَعَثَ أَبَا رَافِعٍ مَوْلَاهُ وَرَجُلًا مِنَ الْأَنْصَارِ فَزَوَّجَاهُ مَيْمُونَةَ، وَالنَّبِيُّ صلى الله عليه وسلم بِالْمَدِينَةِ

  3. 03Malikمَالِكٌ

Report815/ 174
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 180
English
Isnad

Sa'id ibn Maslama informed us, from Isma'il b. Umayya, from Sa'id ibn al-Musayyib, who said:

Matn

“So-and-so was mistaken; the Messenger of God ﷺ married Maymuna only while he was not in ihram.”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا سَعِيدُ بْنُ مَسْلَمَةَ، عَنْ إِسْمَاعِيلَ بْنِ أُمَيَّةَ، عَنْ سَعِيدِ بْنِ الْمُسَيِّبِ قَالَ:

Matn

«وَهِمَ فُلَانٌ، مَا نَكَحَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم مَيْمُونَةَ إِلَّا وَهُوَ حَلَالٌ»

Report816/ 174
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 180
English
Isnad

Sufyan informed us that he heard 'Ubaydullah b. Abi Yazid say: I heard Ibn 'Abbas say: Usama b. Zayd informed me that the Prophet ﷺ said:

Matn

“Usury is only in deferment (al-nasi'a).”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا سُفْيَانُ، أَنَّهُ سَمِعَ عُبَيْدَ اللَّهِ بْنَ أَبِي يَزِيدَ، يَقُولُ: سَمِعْتُ ابْنَ عَبَّاسٍ، يَقُولُ: أَخْبَرَنِي أُسَامَةُ بْنُ زَيْدٍ، أَنَّ النَّبِيَّ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ:

Matn

«إِنَّمَا الرِّبَا فِي النَّسِيئَةِ»

Report817/ 174
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 180
English
Isnad

'Abd al-Wahhab informed us, from Ayyub b. Abi Tamima, from Muhammad b. Sirin, from Muslim b. Yasar and another man, from 'Ubada b. al-Samit ﵁, that the Messenger of God ﷺ said:

Matn

“Do not sell gold for gold, nor silver for silver, nor wheat for wheat, nor barley for barley, nor dates for dates, nor salt for salt, except equal for equal, in kind for kind, hand to hand; but sell [p. 181] gold for silver, and silver for gold, and wheat for barley, and barley for wheat, and dates for salt, and salt for dates, hand to hand, however you wish.” One of the two narrators omitted dates or salt, and one of them added: “Whoever adds, or seeks an increase, has engaged in usury.”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا عَبْدُ الْوَهَّابِ، عَنْ أَيُّوبَ بْنِ أَبِي تَمِيمَةَ، عَنْ مُحَمَّدِ بْنِ سِيرِينَ، عَنْ مُسْلِمِ بْنِ يَسَارٍ، وَرَجُلٍ، آخَرَ عَنْ عُبَادَةَ بْنِ الصَّامِتِ رضي الله عنه، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ:

Matn

«لَا تَبِيعُوا الذَّهَبَ بِالذَّهَبِ، وَلَا الْوَرِقَ بِالْوَرِقِ، وَلَا الْبُرَّ بِالْبُرِّ، وَلَا الشَّعِيرَ بِالشَّعِيرِ، وَلَا التَّمْرَ بِالتَّمْرِ، وَلَا الْمِلْحَ بِالْمِلْحِ إِلَّا سَوَاءً بِسَوَاءٍ، عَيْنًا بِعَيْنٍ، يَدًا بِيَدٍ، وَلَكِنْ بِيعُوا ⦗ص: 181⦘ الذَّهَبَ بِالْوَرِقِ، وَالْوَرِقَ بِالذَّهَبِ، وَالْبُرَّ بِالشَّعِيرِ، وَالشَّعِيرَ بِالْبُرِّ، وَالتَّمْرَ بِالْمِلْحِ، وَالْمِلْحَ بِالتَّمْرِ، يَدًا بِيَدٍ كَيْفَ شِئْتُمْ». وَنَقَصَ أَحَدُهُمَا التَّمْرَ أَوِ الْمِلْحَ، وَزَادَ أَحَدُهُمَا: «مَنْ زَادَ أَوِ ازْدَادَ فَقَدْ أَرْبَى»

Transmission overview

Transmission path

  1. 01'Ubada b. al-SamitEarliest transmitterعُبَادَةَ بْنِ الصَّامِتِ
  2. 02Muslim b. Yasar and another manمُسْلِمِ بْنِ يَسَارٍ، وَرَجُلٍ، آخَرَ
  3. 03Muhammad b. Sirinمُحَمَّدِ بْنِ سِيرِينَ
  4. 04Ayyub b. Abi Tamimaأَيُّوبَ بْنِ أَبِي تَمِيمَةَ
  5. 05'Abd al-Wahhabعَبْدُ الْوَهَّابِ

Report818/ 174
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 181
English
Isnad

Malik informed us, from Musa b. Abi Tamim, from Sa'id ibn Yasar, from Abu Hurayra ﵁, that the Messenger of God ﷺ said:

Matn

“The dinar for the dinar, the dirham for the dirham, with no excess between them.”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا مَالِكٌ، عَنْ مُوسَى بْنِ أَبِي تَمِيمٍ، عَنْ سَعِيدِ بْنِ يَسَارٍ، عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ رضي الله عنه، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ:

Matn

«الدِّينَارُ بِالدِّينَارِ، وَالدِّرْهَمُ بِالدِّرْهَمِ، لَا فَضْلَ بَيْنَهُمَا»

  2. 02Sa'id ibn Yasarسَعِيدِ بْنِ يَسَارٍ

Report819/ 174
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 181
English
Isnad

Malik informed us, from Nafi', from Abu Sa'id al-Khudri, that the Prophet ﷺ said:

Matn

“Do not sell gold for gold except like for like, and do not make any of it exceed another; and do not sell silver for silver except like for like, and do not make any of it exceed another; and do not sell any of it that is absent for something present.”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا مَالِكٌ، عَنْ نَافِعٍ، عَنْ أَبِي سَعِيدٍ الْخُدْرِيِّ، أَنَّ النَّبِيَّ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ:

Matn

«لَا تَبِيعُوا الذَّهَبَ بِالذَّهَبِ إِلَّا مِثْلًا بِمِثْلٍ، وَلَا تُشِفُّوا بَعْضَهَا عَلَى بَعْضٍ، وَلَا تَبِيعُوا الْوَرِقَ بِالْوَرِقِ إِلَّا مِثْلًا بِمِثْلٍ، وَلَا تُشِفُّوا بَعْضَهَا عَلَى بَعْضٍ، وَلَا تَبِيعُوا غَائِبًا مِنْهَا بِنَاجِزٍ»

Transmission overview

1 path

Transmission path

Earliest to latest
  1. 01Abu Sa'id al-KhudriEarliest transmitterأَبِي سَعِيدٍ الْخُدْرِيِّ
  2. 02Nafi'نَافِعٍ
  3. 03Malikمَالِكٌ

Report820/ 174
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 181
English
Isnad

Malik informed us that

Matn

it had reached him, from his grandfather Malik b. Abi 'Amir, from 'Uthman ﵁, who said: The Messenger of God ﷺ said: “Do not sell one dinar for two dinars, nor one dirham for two dirhams.”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا مَالِكٌ،

Matn

أَنَّهُ بَلَغَهُ عَنْ جَدِّهِ، مَالِكِ بْنِ أَبِي عَامِرٍ، عَنْ عُثْمَانَ رضي الله عنه قَالَ: قَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم: «لَا تَبِيعُوا الدِّينَارَ بِالدِّينَارَيْنِ، وَلَا الدِّرْهَمَ بِالدِّرْهَمَيْنِ»

Report821/ 174
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 181
English
Isnad

Malik informed us, from Ibn Shihab, from Sa'id ibn al-Musayyib and Abu Salama b. 'Abd al-Rahman, that the Messenger of God ﷺ said:

Matn

“Pre-emption (shuf'a) applies to what has not been divided; when the boundaries have been set, there is no pre-emption.” The trustworthy one informed us, from Ma'mar, from al-Zuhri, from Abu Salama, from Jabir b. 'Abdullah ﵄, from the Messenger of God ﷺ, the same or the same in meaning, not contradicting it.

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا مَالِكٌ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، عَنْ سَعِيدِ بْنِ الْمُسَيِّبِ، وَأَبِي سَلَمَةَ بْنِ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ:

Matn

«الشُّفْعَةُ فِيمَا لَمْ يُقْسَمْ، فَإِذَا وَقَعَتِ الْحُدُودُ فَلَا شُفْعَةَ» أَخْبَرَنَا الثِّقَةُ، عَنْ مَعْمَرٍ، عَنِ الزُّهْرِيِّ، عَنْ أَبِي سَلَمَةَ، عَنْ جَابِرِ بْنِ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ رضي الله عنهما، عَنْ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم مِثْلَهُ أَوْ مِثْلَ مَعْنَاهُ لَا يُخَالِفُهُ

  2. 02Ibn Shihabابْنِ شِهَابٍ
  3. 03Malikمَالِكٌ

Report822/ 174
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 181
English
Isnad

Sa'id ibn Salim informed us: Ibn Jurayj informed us, from Abu al-Zubayr, from Jabir ﵁, from the Prophet ﷺ, that he said:

Matn

“Pre-emption applies to what has not been divided; when the boundaries have been set, there is no pre-emption.”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا سَعِيدُ بْنُ سَالِمٍ، أَخْبَرَنَا ابْنُ جُرَيْجٍ، عَنْ أَبِي الزُّبَيْرِ، عَنْ جَابِرٍ رضي الله عنه، عَنِ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم أَنَّهُ قَالَ:

Matn

«الشُّفْعَةُ فِيمَا لَمْ يُقْسَمْ، فَإِذَا وَقَعَتِ الْحُدُودُ فَلَا شُفْعَةَ»

Report823/ 174
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 182
English
Isnad

Al-Shafi'i informed us, saying:

Matn

Sufyan informed him, from Ibrahim b. Maysara, from 'Amr b. al-Sharid, from Abu Rafi', that the Messenger of God ﷺ said: “The neighbor has more right to his adjoining property.”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا الشَّافِعِيُّ قَالَ:

Matn

فَإِنَّ سُفْيَانَ أَخْبَرَهُ عَنْ إِبْرَاهِيمَ بْنِ مَيْسَرَةَ، عَنْ عَمْرِو بْنِ الشَّرِيدِ، عَنْ أَبِي رَافِعٍ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ: «الْجَارُ أَحَقُّ بِسَقَبِهِ»

Report824/ 174
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 182
English
Isnad

Malik b. Anas informed us, from 'Abdullah b. Abi Bakr, from his father, from 'Amra, that she heard 'A'isha ﵂, when it was mentioned to her that 'Abdullah b. 'Umar says:

Matn

“The dead is tormented by the weeping of the living.” 'A'isha said: He did not lie, but he erred or forgot. The Messenger of God ﷺ only passed by a Jewish woman whose family were weeping over her, and said: “They are weeping over her while she is being tormented in her grave.”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا مَالِكُ بْنُ أَنَسٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ أَبِي بَكْرٍ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، عَنْ عَمْرَةَ، أَنَّهَا سَمِعَتْ عَائِشَةَ رضي الله عنها وَذُكِرَ لَهَا أَنَّ عَبْدَ اللَّهِ بْنَ عُمَرَ يَقُولُ:

Matn

إِنَّ الْمَيِّتِ لَيُعَذَّبُ بِبُكَاءِ الْحَيِّ، فَقَالَتْ عَائِشَةُ: أَمَا إِنَّهُ لَمْ يَكْذِبْ، وَلَكِنَّهُ أَخْطَأَ أَوْ نَسِيَ، إِنَّمَا مَرَّ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم عَلَى يَهُودِيَّةٍ وَهِيَ يَبْكِي عَلَيْهَا أَهْلُهَا فَقَالَ: «إِنَّهُمْ لَيَبْكُونَ عَلَيْهَا وَإِنَّهَا لَتُعَذَّبُ فِي قَبْرِهَا»

Report825/ 174
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 182
English
Isnad

'Abd al-Majid b. 'Abd al-'Aziz informed us, from Ibn Jurayj: Ibn Abi Mulayka informed me, saying:

Matn

A daughter of 'Uthman b. 'Affan died in Mecca, and we came to attend her (burial); Ibn 'Abbas and Ibn 'Umar were present. He said: I was sitting between the two of them—I had sat beside one of them and then the other came and sat beside me—and Ibn 'Umar said to 'Amr b. 'Uthman: Will you not forbid weeping? For the Messenger of God ﷺ said: “The dead is tormented by his family's weeping over him.” Ibn 'Abbas said: 'Umar used to say some of that. Then Ibn 'Abbas narrated, saying: I set out with 'Umar b. al-Khattab from Mecca, until when we were at al-Bayda' there was a party of riders under the shade of a tree. He said: Go and see who these riders are. I went, and it was Suhayb. He said: Call him. I returned to Suhayb and said: Mount and join the Commander of the Believers. When 'Umar was wounded, I heard Suhayb weeping, saying: Alas, my brother! Alas, my companion! 'Umar said: O Suhayb, do you weep for me, when the Messenger of God ﷺ said: “The dead is tormented by his family's weeping over him”? He said: When 'Umar died, I mentioned that to 'A'isha, and she said: God have mercy on 'Umar. No, by God, the Messenger of God ﷺ did not narrate that God torments the believer for his family's weeping over him; rather the Messenger of God ﷺ said: “God increases the unbeliever in torment through his family's weeping over him.” 'A'isha said: The Qur'an is sufficient for you: {No bearer of burdens bears the burden of another} [al-An'am: 164]. And Ibn 'Abbas ﵄ said at that: And God it is who makes laugh and makes weep. Ibn Abi [p. 183] Mulayka said: By God, Ibn 'Umar said nothing.

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا عَبْدُ الْمَجِيدِ بْنُ عَبْدِ الْعَزِيزِ، عَنِ ابْنِ جُرَيْجٍ، أَخْبَرَنِي ابْنُ أَبِي مُلَيْكَةَ قَالَ:

Matn

تُوُفِّيَتِ ابْنَةٌ لِعُثْمَانَ بْنِ عَفَّانَ بِمَكَّةَ فَجِئْنَا نَشْهَدُهَا، وَحَضَرَهَا ابْنُ عَبَّاسٍ وَابْنُ عُمَرَ، فَقَالَ: إِنِّي لَجَالِسٌ بَيْنَهُمَا جَلَسْتُ إِلَى أَحَدِهِمَا ثُمَّ جَاءَ الْآخَرُ فَجَلَسَ إِلَيَّ فَقَالَ ابْنُ عُمَرَ لِعَمْرِو بْنِ عُثْمَانَ: أَلَا تَنْهَى عَنِ الْبُكَاءِ؛ فَإِنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ: «إِنَّ الْمَيِّتَ لَيُعَذَّبُ بِبُكَاءِ أَهْلِهِ عَلَيْهِ». فَقَالَ ابْنُ عَبَّاسٍ: قَدْ كَانَ عُمَرُ يَقُولُ بَعْضَ ذَلِكَ، ثُمَّ حَدَّثَ ابْنُ عَبَّاسٍ قَالَ: صَدَرْتُ مَعَ عُمَرَ بْنِ الْخَطَّابِ مِنْ مَكَّةَ حَتَّى إِذَا كُنَّا بِالْبَيْدَاءِ إِذَا بِرَكْبٍ تَحْتَ ظِلِّ شَجَرَةٍ، قَالَ: اذْهَبْ فَانْظُرْ مَنْ هَؤُلَاءِ الرَّكْبُ، فَذَهَبْتُ فَإِذَا صُهَيْبٌ، قَالَ: ادْعُهُ، فَرَجَعْتُ إِلَى صُهَيْبٍ فَقُلْتُ: ارْتَحِلْ فَالْحَقْ بِأَمِيرِ الْمُؤْمِنِينَ، فَلَمَّا أُصِيبَ عُمَرُ سَمِعْتُ صُهَيْبًا يَبْكِي وَهُوَ يَقُولُ: وَاأُخَيَّاهُ، وَاصَاحِبَاهُ، فَقَالَ عُمَرُ: يَا صُهَيْبُ، أَتَبْكِي عَلَيَّ وَقَدْ قَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم: «إِنَّ الْمَيِّتَ لَيُعَذَّبُ بِبُكَاءِ أَهْلِهِ عَلَيْهِ». قَالَ: فَلَمَّا مَاتَ عُمَرُ ذَكَرْتُ ذَلِكَ لِعَائِشَةَ فَقَالَتْ: يَرْحَمُ اللَّهُ عُمَرَ، لَا وَاللَّهِ مَا حَدَّثَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم أَنَّ اللَّهَ يُعَذِّبُ الْمُؤْمِنَ بِبُكَاءِ أَهْلِهِ عَلَيْهِ، وَلَكِنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ: «إِنَّ اللَّهَ يَزِيدُ الْكَافِرَ عَذَابًا بِبُكَاءِ أَهْلِهِ عَلَيْهِ». فَقَالَتْ عَائِشَةُ: حَسْبُكُمُ الْقُرْآنُ: {لَا تَزِرُ وَازِرَةٌ وِزْرَ أُخْرَى} [الْأَنْعَام: 164] وَقَالَ ابْنُ عَبَّاسٍ رضي الله عنهما عِنْدَ ذَلِكَ: وَاللَّهُ أَضْحَكَ وَأَبْكَى. قَالَ ابْنُ أَبِي ⦗ص: 183⦘ مُلَيْكَةَ: فَوَاللَّهِ مَا قَالَ ابْنُ عُمَرَ مِنْ شَيْءٍ

Report826/ 174
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 183
English
Isnad

Sufyan informed us, from al-Zuhri, from 'Ata' b. Yazid al-Laythi, from Abu Ayyub al-Ansari ﵁, from the Prophet ﷺ, that

Matn

he forbade facing the qibla for defecation or urination, “but face east or west.” He said: We came to Syria and found latrines that had been built facing the qibla, so we turn aside and seek the forgiveness of God Almighty.

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا سُفْيَانُ، عَنِ الزُّهْرِيِّ، عَنْ عَطَاءِ بْنِ يَزِيدَ اللَّيْثِيِّ، عَنْ أَبِي أَيُّوبَ الْأَنْصَارِيِّ رضي الله عنه عَنِ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم

Matn

أَنَّهُ نَهَى أَنْ تُسْتَقْبَلَ الْقِبْلَةُ بِغَائِطٍ أَوْ بَوْلٍ، وَلَكِنْ شَرِّقُوا أَوْ غَرِّبُوا، قَالَ: فَقَدِمْنَا الشَّامَ فَوَجَدْنَا مَرَاحِيضَ قَدْ بُنِيَتْ قِبَلَ الْقِبْلَةِ فَنَنْحَرِفُ وَنَسْتَغْفِرُ اللَّهَ تَعَالَى

Report827/ 174
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 183
English
Isnad

Malik informed us, from Yahya b. Sa'id, from Muhammad b. Yahya b. Hibban, from his paternal uncle Wasi', from 'Abdullah b. 'Umar ﵄, that

Matn

he used to say: People say: When you sit for your need, do not face the qibla nor Bayt al-Maqdis. 'Abdullah b. 'Umar said: “I climbed up onto the roof of a house of ours and saw the Messenger of God ﷺ on two bricks, facing Bayt al-Maqdis, for his need.”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا مَالِكٌ، عَنْ يَحْيَى بْنِ سَعِيدٍ، عَنْ مُحَمَّدِ بْنِ يَحْيَى بْنِ حَبَّانَ، عَنْ عَمِّهِ وَاسِعِ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عُمَرَ رضي الله عنهما

Matn

أَنَّهُ كَانَ يَقُولُ: إِنَّ نَاسًا يَقُولُونَ: إِذَا قَعَدْتَ عَلَى حَاجَتِكَ فَلَا تَسْتَقْبِلِ الْقِبْلَةَ وَلَا بَيْتَ الْمَقْدِسِ. قَالَ عَبْدُ اللَّهِ بْنُ عُمَرَ: لَقَدِ «ارْتَقَيْتُ عَلَى ظَهْرِ بَيْتٍ لَنَا فَرَأَيْتُ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم عَلَى لَبِنَتَيْنِ مُسْتَقْبِلًا بَيْتَ الْمَقْدِسِ لِحَاجَتِهِ»

Report828/ 174
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 183
English
Isnad

Sufyan b. 'Uyayna informed us, from al-Zuhri, from Abu al-Zinad, from al-A'raj, from Abu Hurayra ﵁, that the Messenger of God ﷺ said:

Matn

“Let none of you pray in a single garment with nothing of it upon his shoulders.”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا سُفْيَانُ بْنُ عُيَيْنَةَ، عَنِ الزُّهْرِيِّ، عَنْ أَبِي الزِّنَادِ، عَنِ الْأَعْرَجِ، عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ، رَضِيَ اللَّهُ تَعَالَى عَنْهُ أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ:

Matn

«لَا يُصَلِّيَنَّ أَحَدُكُمْ فِي الثَّوْبِ الْوَاحِدِ لَيْسَ عَلَى عَاتِقَيْهِ مِنْهُ شَيْءٌ»

Transmission overview

Transmission path

Earliest to latest

  2. 02al-A'rajالْأَعْرَجِ
  3. 03Abu al-Zinadأَبِي الزِّنَادِ
  4. 04al-Zuhriالزُّهْرِيِّ
  5. 05Sufyan b. 'Uyaynaسُفْيَانُ بْنُ عُيَيْنَةَ

Report829/ 174
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 183
English
Isnad

Sufyan informed us, from Abu Ishaq, from 'Abdullah b. Shaddad, from Maymuna ﵂, wife of the Prophet ﷺ, who said:

Matn

The Messenger of God ﷺ used to pray in a wrap, part of it on me and part of it on him, while I was menstruating.

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا سُفْيَانُ، عَنْ أَبِي إِسْحَاقَ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ شَدَّادٍ، عَنْ مَيْمُونَةَ رضي الله عنها زَوْجِ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَتْ:

Matn

كَانَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم يُصَلِّي فِي مِرْطٍ بَعْضُهُ عَلَيَّ وَبَعْضُهُ عَلَيْهِ وَأَنَا حَائِضٌ

Transmission overview

1 path

Transmission path

Earliest to latest
  1. 01Maymuna ﵂, wife of the ProphetEarliest transmitterمَيْمُونَةَ
  2. 02'Abdullah b. Shaddadعَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ شَدَّادٍ
  3. 03Abu Ishaqأَبِي إِسْحَاقَ
  4. 04Sufyanسُفْيَانُ

Report830/ 174
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 183
English
Isnad

Sufyan informed us, from 'Asim b. Abi al-Najud, from Abu Wa'il, from 'Abdullah ﵁, who said:

Matn

We used to greet the Prophet ﷺ while he was in prayer, before we went to the land of Abyssinia, and he would return our greeting while he was in prayer. When we returned from the land of Abyssinia I came to greet him and found him praying; I greeted him and he did not reply to me, and I was seized by thoughts near and far. I sat until, when he had finished [p. 184] his prayer, I came to him, and he said: “God, glorified be His mention, brings about whatever He wills of His command, and among what God has newly brought about is that you not speak in prayer.”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا سُفْيَانُ، عَنْ عَاصِمِ بْنِ أَبِي النَّجُودِ، عَنْ أَبِي وَائِلٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ رضي الله عنه قَالَ:

Matn

كُنَّا نُسَلِّمُ عَلَى النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم وَهُوَ فِي الصَّلَاةِ قَبْلَ أَنْ نَأْتِيَ أَرْضَ الْحَبَشَةِ فَيَرُدُّ عَلَيْنَا وَهُوَ فِي الصَّلَاةِ، فَلَمَّا رَجَعْنَا مِنْ أَرْضِ الْحَبَشَةِ أَتَيْتُهُ لِأُسَلِّمَ عَلَيْهِ فَوَجَدْتُهُ يُصَلِّي فَسَلَّمْتُ عَلَيْهِ فَلَمْ يَرُدَّ عَلَيَّ، فَأَخَذَنِي مَا قَرُبَ وَمَا بَعُدَ، فَجَلَسْتُ حَتَّى إِذَا قَضَى ⦗ص: 184⦘ صَلَاتَهُ أَتَيْتُهُ فَقَالَ: «إِنَّ اللَّهَ جَلَّ ثَنَاؤُهُ يُحْدِثُ مِنْ أَمْرِهِ مَا يَشَاءُ، وَإِنَّ مِمَّا أَحْدَثَ اللَّهُ أَنْ لَا تَكَلَّمُوا فِي الصَّلَاةِ»

  2. 02Abu Wa'ilأَبِي وَائِلٍ
  3. 03'Asim b. Abi al-Najudعَاصِمِ بْنِ أَبِي النَّجُودِ
  4. 04Sufyanسُفْيَانُ

Report831/ 174
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 184
English
Isnad

Malik informed us, from Ayyub al-Sakhtiyani, from Muhammad b. Sirin, from Abu Hurayra ﵁, that

Matn

the Messenger of God ﷺ finished (the prayer) after two (rak'as), and Dhu al-Yadayn said: Has the prayer been shortened, or have you forgotten, O Messenger of God? The Messenger of God ﷺ said: “Has Dhu al-Yadayn spoken truly?” The people said: Yes. So the Messenger of God ﷺ stood and prayed two more, then gave the salam, then said the takbir and prostrated like his (usual) prostration or longer, then rose, then said the takbir and prostrated like his prostration or longer, then rose.

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا مَالِكٌ، عَنْ أَيُّوبَ السَّخْتِيَانِيِّ، عَنْ مُحَمَّدِ بْنِ سِيرِينَ، عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ رضي الله عنه،

Matn

أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم انْصَرَفَ مِنَ اثْنَتَيْنِ، فَقَالَ ذُو الْيَدَيْنِ: أَقُصِرَتِ الصَّلَاةُ أَمْ نَسِيتَ يَا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ؟ فَقَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم: «أَصَدَقَ ذُو الْيَدَيْنِ؟» فَقَالَ النَّاسُ: نَعَمْ، فَقَامَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم فَصَلَّى اثْنَتَيْنِ أُخْرَيَيْنِ ثُمَّ سَلَّمَ، ثُمَّ كَبَّرَ فَسَجَدَ مِثْلَ سُجُودِهِ أَوْ أَطْوَلَ، ثُمَّ رَفَعَ، ثُمَّ كَبَّرَ فَسَجَدَ مِثْلَ سُجُودِهِ أَوْ أَطْوَلَ، ثُمَّ رَفَعَ

Transmission overview

1 path

Transmission path

Earliest to latest
  1. 01Abu HurayraEarliest transmitterأَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ
  2. 02Muhammad b. Sirinمُحَمَّدِ بْنِ سِيرِينَ
  3. 03Ayyub al-Sakhtiyaniأَيُّوبَ السَّخْتِيَانِيِّ
  4. 04Malikمَالِكٌ

Report832/ 174
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 184
English
Isnad

Malik informed us, from Dawud b. al-Husayn, from Abu Sufyan, the client of Ibn Abi Ahmad, who said: I heard Abu Hurayra ﵁ say:

Matn

The Messenger of God ﷺ led us in a prayer, (stopping) at two rak'as, and Dhu al-Yadayn stood and said: Has the prayer been shortened, or have you forgotten, O Messenger of God? The Messenger of God ﷺ turned to the people and said: “Has Dhu al-Yadayn spoken truly?” They said: Yes. So the Messenger of God ﷺ completed what remained of the prayer, and then prostrated two prostrations while sitting after the salam.

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا مَالِكٌ، عَنْ دَاوُدَ بْنِ الْحُصَيْنِ، عَنْ أَبِي سُفْيَانَ، مَوْلَى ابْنِ أَبِي أَحْمَدَ قَالَ: سَمِعْتُ أَبَا هُرَيْرَةَ رضي الله عنه يَقُولُ:

Matn

صَلَّى لَنَا رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم صَلَاةً فِي رَكْعَتَيْنِ، فَقَامَ ذُو الْيَدَيْنِ فَقَالَ: أَقُصِرَتِ الصَّلَاةُ أَمْ نَسِيتَ يَا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ؟ فَأَقْبَلَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم عَلَى النَّاسِ فَقَالَ: «أَصَدَقَ ذُو الْيَدَيْنِ؟» فَقَالُوا: نَعَمْ، فَأَتَمَّ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم مَا بَقِيَ مِنَ الصَّلَاةِ ثُمَّ سَجَدَ سَجْدَتَيْنِ وَهُوَ جَالِسٌ بَعْدَ التَّسْلِيمِ

Transmission overview

1 path

Transmission path

Earliest to latest
  1. 01Abu HurayraEarliest transmitterأَبَا هُرَيْرَةَ
  2. 02Abu Sufyan, the client of Ibn Abi Ahmadأَبِي سُفْيَانَ، مَوْلَى ابْنِ أَبِي أَحْمَدَ
  3. 03Dawud b. al-Husaynدَاوُدَ بْنِ الْحُصَيْنِ
  4. 04Malikمَالِكٌ

Report833/ 174
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 184
English
Isnad

'Abd al-Wahhab al-Thaqafi informed us, from Khalid al-Hadhdha', from Abu Qilaba, from Abu al-Muhallab, from 'Imran b. Husayn, who said:

Matn

The Prophet ﷺ gave the salam after three rak'as of 'Asr, then stood and entered the room; al-Khirbaq, a man with long arms, stood and called out: O Messenger of God, has the prayer been shortened? So he came out angry, dragging his cloak, asked, and was informed; he prayed that rak'a that he had omitted, then gave the salam, then prostrated two prostrations, then gave the salam.

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا عَبْدُ الْوَهَّابِ الثَّقَفِيُّ، عَنْ خَالِدٍ الْحَذَّاءِ، عَنْ أَبِي قِلَابَةَ، عَنْ أَبِي الْمُهَلَّبِ، عَنْ عِمْرَانَ بْنِ حُصَيْنٍ قَالَ:

Matn

سَلَّمَ النَّبِيُّ صلى الله عليه وسلم فِي ثَلَاثِ رَكَعَاتٍ مِنَ الْعَصْرِ، ثُمَّ قَامَ فَدَخَلَ الْحُجْرَةَ، فَقَامَ الْخِرْبَاقُ رَجُلٌ بَسِيطُ الْيَدَيْنِ فَنَادَى: يَا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ، أَقُصِرَتِ الصَّلَاةُ؟ فَخَرَجَ مُغْضَبًا يَجُرُّ رِدَاءَهُ فَسَأَلَ، فَأُخْبِرَ، فَصَلَّى تِلْكَ الرَّكْعَةَ الَّتِي كَانَ تَرَكَ، ثُمَّ سَلَّمَ، ثُمَّ سَجَدَ سَجْدَتَيْنِ، ثُمَّ سَلَّمَ

Transmission path

Earliest to latest
  1. 01'Imran b. HusaynEarliest transmitterعِمْرَانَ بْنِ حُصَيْنٍ
  2. 02Abu al-Muhallabأَبِي الْمُهَلَّبِ
  3. 03Abu Qilabaأَبِي قِلَابَةَ

Report834/ 174
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 184
English
Isnad

Some of the people of knowledge informed me, from Ja'far b. Muhammad, from his father, that he said:

Matn

When the killing of the people of Bi'r Ma'una reached the Messenger of God ﷺ, he remained for fifteen nights, each time he raised his head from the last rak'a of the dawn prayer saying: “God hears whoever praises Him; our Lord, to You is the praise. O God, do (so and so).” Then he mentioned [p. 185] a long supplication, then said the takbir and prostrated.

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنِي بَعْضُ أَهْلِ الْعِلْمِ، عَنْ جَعْفَرِ بْنِ مُحَمَّدٍ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، أَنَّهُ قَالَ:

Matn

لَمَّا انْتَهَى إِلَى رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَتْلُ أَهْلِ بِئْرِ مَعُونَةَ أَقَامَ خَمْسَ عَشْرَةَ لَيْلَةً كُلَّمَا رَفَعَ رَأْسَهُ مِنَ الرَّكْعَةِ الْآخِرَةِ مِنَ الصُّبْحِ قَالَ: «سَمِعَ اللَّهُ لِمَنْ حَمِدَهُ، رَبَّنَا لَكَ الْحَمْدُ، اللَّهُمَّ افْعَلْ». فَذَكَرَ ⦗ص: 185⦘ دُعَاءً طَوِيلًا، ثُمَّ كَبَّرَ فَسَجَدَ

Transmission path

Earliest to latest
  1. 01his fatherEarliest transmitterأَبِيهِ
  2. 02Ja'far b. Muhammadجَعْفَرِ بْنِ مُحَمَّدٍ
  3. 03Some of the people of knowledgeبَعْضُ أَهْلِ الْعِلْمِ

Report835/ 174
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 185
English
Isnad

Sufyan informed us, from al-Zuhri, from Ibn al-Musayyib, from Abu Hurayra ﵁, that when the Prophet ﷺ raised his head from the second rak'a of the dawn prayer he said:

Matn

“O God, rescue al-Walid b. al-Walid, Salama b. Hisham, 'Ayyash b. Abi Rabi'a, and the oppressed in Mecca. O God, press down hard upon Mudar, and make it for them years like the years of Joseph.”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا سُفْيَانُ، عَنِ الزُّهْرِيِّ، عَنِ ابْنِ الْمُسَيِّبِ، عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ رضي الله عنه، أَنَّ النَّبِيَّ صلى الله عليه وسلم لَمَّا رَفَعَ رَأْسَهُ مِنَ الرَّكْعَةِ الثَّانِيَةِ مِنَ الصُّبْحِ قَالَ:

Matn

«اللَّهُمَّ أَنْجِ الْوَلِيدَ بْنَ الْوَلِيدِ، وَسَلَمَةَ بْنَ هِشَامٍ، وَعَيَّاشَ بْنَ أَبِي رَبِيعَةَ، وَالْمُسْتَضْعَفِينَ بِمَكَّةَ، اللَّهُمَّ اشْدُدْ وَطْأَتَكَ عَلَى مُضَرَ، وَاجْعَلْهَا عَلَيْهِمْ سِنِينَ كَسِنِي يُوسُفَ»

  3. 03English form not separately alignedالزُّهْرِيِّ
  4. 04English form not separately alignedسُفْيَانُ

Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 185
English
Isnad

Sufyan informed us, from 'Amr b. Dinar, from Salim b. 'Abdullah—and sometimes he said: from his father, and sometimes he said:

Matn

'Umar said: “When you have stoned the jamra, sacrificed, and shaved, everything that was forbidden to you has become lawful for you except women and perfume.” Salim said: And 'A'isha said: “I perfumed the Messenger of God ﷺ for his ihram before he entered ihram, and for his release after he stoned the jamra and before he visited the House.” Salim ﵁ said: “And the sunna of the Messenger of God ﷺ is more deserving to be followed.”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا سُفْيَانُ، عَنْ عَمْرِو بْنِ دِينَارٍ، عَنْ سَالِمِ بْنِ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ، وَرُبَّمَا، قَالَ: عَنْ أَبِيهِ، وَرُبَّمَا، قَالَ:

Matn

قَالَ عُمَرُ: «إِذَا رَمَيْتُمُ الْجَمْرَةَ وَذَبَحْتُمْ وَحَلَقْتُمْ فَقَدْ حَلَّ لَكُمْ كُلُّ شَيْءٍ حَرُمَ عَلَيْكُمْ إِلَّا النِّسَاءَ وَالطِّيبَ» قَالَ سَالِمٌ: وَقَالَتْ عَائِشَةُ: «أَنَا طَيَّبْتُ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم لِإِحْرَامِهِ قَبْلَ أَنْ يُحْرِمَ وَلِحِلِّهِ بَعْدَ أَنْ رَمَى الْجَمْرَةَ وَقَبْلَ أَنْ يَزُورَ الْبَيْتَ» قَالَ سَالِمٌ رضي الله عنه: «وَسُنَّةُ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم أَحَقُّ أَنْ تُتَّبَعَ»

  3. 03'Amr b. Dinarعَمْرِو بْنِ دِينَارٍ
  4. 04Sufyanسُفْيَانُ

Report837/ 174
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 185
English
Isnad

Malik informed us, from al-Zuhri, from 'Ubaydullah, from Ibn 'Abbas, from al-Sa'b b. Jaththama, that

Matn

he gave a wild donkey as a gift to the Messenger of God ﷺ while he was at al-Abwa' or at Waddan, and the Messenger of God ﷺ returned it to him; when the Messenger of God ﷺ saw what was in his face, he said: “We did not return it to you except because we are in ihram.”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا مَالِكٌ، عَنِ الزُّهْرِيِّ، عَنْ عُبَيْدِ اللَّهِ، عَنِ ابْنِ عَبَّاسٍ، عَنِ الصَّعْبِ بْنِ جَثَّامَةَ،

Matn

أَنَّهُ أَهْدَى لِرَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم حِمَارًا وَحْشِيًّا وَهُوَ بِالْأَبْوَاءِ أَوْ بِوَدَّانَ فَرَدَّهُ عَلَيْهِ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم، فَلَمَّا رَأَى رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم مَا فِي وَجْهِهِ قَالَ: «إِنَّا لَمْ نَرُدَّهُ عَلَيْكَ إِلَّا أَنَّا حُرُمٌ»

Transmission overview

Transmission path

  2. 02Ibn 'Abbasابْنِ عَبَّاسٍ
  3. 03'Ubaydullahعُبَيْدِ اللَّهِ
  4. 04al-Zuhriالزُّهْرِيِّ
  5. 05Malikمَالِكٌ

Report838/ 174
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 185
English
Isnad

Muslim and Sa'id ibn Salim informed us, from Ibn Jurayj; and Malik informed me, from Abu al-Nadr, the client of 'Umar b. 'Ubaydullah al-Taymi, from Nafi', the client of Abu Qatada, from Abu Qatada al-Ansari ﵁, that

Matn

he was with the Prophet ﷺ until, on part of the road to Mecca, he lagged behind with companions of his who were in ihram while he was not in ihram. He saw a wild donkey, mounted his horse, and asked his companions to hand him his whip, but they refused; he asked them for his spear, and they refused; so he took his spear, charged at the donkey and killed it. Some of the Companions of the Prophet ﷺ ate of it and some refused; and when they caught up with the Prophet ﷺ [p. 186] they asked him about that, and he said: “It is only a meal that God Almighty fed you.” Malik informed us, from Zayd b. Aslam, from 'Ata' b. Yasar, from Abu Qatada, concerning the wild donkey, like the hadith of Abu al-Nadr, except that in the hadith of Zayd, the Messenger of God ﷺ said: “Do you have any of its meat with you?”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا مُسْلِمٌ، وَسَعِيدُ بْنُ سَالِمٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ جُرَيْجٍ، وَأَخْبَرَنِي مَالِكٌ، عَنْ أَبِي النَّضْرِ، مَوْلَى عُمَرَ بْنِ عُبَيْدِ اللَّهِ التَّيْمِيِّ، عَنْ نَافِعٍ، مَوْلَى أَبِي قَتَادَةَ، عَنْ أَبِي قَتَادَةَ الْأَنْصَارِيِّ رضي الله عنه

Matn

أَنَّهُ كَانَ مَعَ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم حَتَّى إِذَا كَانَ بِبَعْضِ طَرِيقِ مَكَّةَ تَخَلَّفَ مَعَ أَصْحَابٍ لَهُ مُحْرِمِينَ وَهُوَ غَيْرُ مُحْرِمٍ، فَرَأَى حِمَارًا وَحْشِيًّا فَاسْتَوَى عَلَى فَرَسِهِ فَسَأَلَ أَصْحَابَهُ أَنْ يُنَاوِلُوهُ سَوْطَهُ فَأَبَوْا، فَسَأَلَهُمْ رُمْحَهُ فَأَبَوْا، فَأَخَذَ رُمْحَهُ فَشَدَّ عَلَى الْحِمَارِ فَقَتَلَهُ، فَأَكَلَ مِنْهُ بَعْضُ أَصْحَابِ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم وَأَبَى بَعْضُهُمْ، فَلَمَّا أَدْرَكُوا النَّبِيَّ صلى الله عليه وسلم ⦗ص: 186⦘ سَأَلُوهُ عَنْ ذَلِكَ فَقَالَ: «إِنَّمَا هِيَ طُعْمَةٌ أَطْعَمَكُوُهَا اللَّهُ تَعَالَى» أَخْبَرَنَا مَالِكٌ، عَنْ زَيْدِ بْنِ أَسْلَمَ، عَنْ عَطَاءِ بْنِ يَسَارٍ، عَنْ أَبِي قَتَادَةَ، فِي الْحِمَارِ الْوَحْشِيِّ مِثْلَ حَدِيثِ أَبِي النَّضْرِ، إِلَّا أَنَّ فِي، حَدِيثِ زَيْدٍ أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ: «هَلْ مَعَكُمْ مِنْ لَحْمِهِ مِنْ شَيْءٍ»

  2. 02Nafi', the client of Abu Qatadaنَافِعٍ، مَوْلَى أَبِي قَتَادَةَ
  3. 03Abu al-Nadr, the client of 'Umar b. 'Ubaydullah al-Taymiأَبِي النَّضْرِ، مَوْلَى عُمَرَ بْنِ عُبَيْدِ اللَّهِ التَّيْمِيِّ
  4. 04and Malikمَالِكٌ

Report839/ 174
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 186
English
Isnad

Ibrahim b. Muhammad informed us, from 'Amr b. Abi 'Amr, from al-Muttalib b. Hantab, from Jabir b. 'Abdullah ﵄, that the Messenger of God ﷺ said:

Matn

“The meat of game is lawful for you while in ihram, so long as you did not hunt it or it was not hunted for you.” One who heard Sulayman narrating from 'Amr b. Abi 'Amr informed us, with this chain, from the Prophet ﷺ, thus. 'Abd al-'Aziz b. Muhammad al-Darawardi informed us, from 'Amr b. Abi 'Amr, from a man of Banu Salama, from Jabir ﵁, from the Prophet ﷺ, thus. Al-Shafi'i ﵁ said: “And Ibn Abi Yahya is a better memorizer than al-Darawardi, and Sulayman is with Ibn Abi Yahya.”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا إِبْرَاهِيمُ بْنُ مُحَمَّدٍ، عَنْ عَمْرِو بْنِ أَبِي عَمْرٍو، عَنِ الْمُطَّلِبِ بْنِ حَنْطَبٍ، عَنْ جَابِرِ بْنِ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ رضي الله عنهما أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ:

Matn

«لَحْمُ الصَّيْدِ لَكُمْ فِي الْإِحْرَامِ حَلَالٌ مَا لَمْ تَصِيدُوهْ أَوْ يُصَادَ لَكُمْ» أَخْبَرَنَا مَنْ، سَمِعَ سُلَيْمَانَ، يُحَدِّثُ عَنْ عَمْرِو بْنِ أَبِي عَمْرٍو، وَبِهَذَا الْإِسْنَادِ، عَنِ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم هَكَذَا أَخْبَرَنَا عَبْدُ الْعَزِيزِ بْنُ مُحَمَّدٍ الدَّرَاوَرْدِيُّ، عَنْ عَمْرِو بْنِ أَبِي عَمْرٍو، عَنْ رَجُلٍ، مِنْ بَنِي سَلَمَةَ عَنْ جَابِرٍ رضي الله عنه، عَنِ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم هَكَذَا قَالَ الشَّافِعِيُّ رضي الله عنه: «وَابْنُ أَبِي يَحْيَى أَحْفَظُ مِنَ الدَّرَاوَرْدِيِّ، وَسُلَيْمَانُ مَعَ ابْنِ أَبِي يَحْيَى»

  3. 03'Amr b. Abi 'Amrعَمْرِو بْنِ أَبِي عَمْرٍو

Report840/ 174
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 186
English
Isnad

Malik informed us, from Nafi', from Ibn 'Umar ﵄, that the Messenger of God ﷺ said:

Matn

“Let none of you propose over the proposal of his brother.” Malik informed us, from Abu al-Zinad, from al-A'raj, from Abu Hurayra ﵁, from the Prophet ﷺ, the same. And some of the hadith narrators have added: “until he abandons it or gives permission.”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا مَالِكٌ، عَنْ نَافِعٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ عُمَرَ رضي الله عنهما أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ:

Matn

«لَا يَخْطُبُ أَحَدُكُمْ عَلَى خِطْبَةِ أَخِيهِ» أَخْبَرَنَا مَالِكٌ، عَنْ أَبِي الزِّنَادِ، عَنِ الْأَعْرَجِ، عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ رضي الله عنه، عَنِ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم مِثْلَهُ. وَقَدْ زَادَ بَعْضُ الْمُحَدِّثِينَ: حَتَّى يَتْرُكَ أَوْ يَأْذَنَ

Transmission path

  2. 02Nafi'نَافِعٍ
  3. 03Malikمَالِكٌ

Report841/ 174
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 186
English
Isnad

Malik informed us, from 'Abdullah b. Yazid, the client of al-Aswad b. Sufyan, from Abu Salama b. 'Abd al-Rahman, from Fatima, that

Matn

the Messenger of God ﷺ said to her during her waiting period after her husband's divorce [p. 187] of her: “When you are free (to marry), let me know.” She said: When I was free, I informed him that Mu'awiya and Abu Jahm had proposed to me. The Messenger of God ﷺ said: “As for Mu'awiya, he is a poor man with no money; and as for Abu Jahm, he does not put his staff down from his shoulder. Marry Usama.” She said: But I disliked him. He said: “Marry Usama.” So I married him, and God put good in him, and I was glad of him.

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا مَالِكٌ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ يَزِيدَ، مَوْلَى الْأَسْوَدِ بْنِ سُفْيَانَ، عَنْ أَبِي سَلَمَةَ بْنِ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ، عَنْ فَاطِمَةَ،

Matn

أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ لَهَا فِي عِدَّتِهَا مِنْ طَلَاقِ ⦗ص: 187⦘ زَوْجِهَا: «فَإِذَا حَلَلْتَ فَآذِنِينِي». قَالَتْ: فَلَمَّا حَلَلْتُ أَخْبَرْتُهُ أَنَّ مُعَاوِيَةَ وَأَبَا جَهْمٍ خَطَبَانِي، فَقَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم: «أَمَّا مُعَاوِيَةُ فَصُعْلُوكٌ لَا مَالَ لَهُ، وَأَمَّا أَبُو جَهْمٍ فَلَا يَضَعُ عَصَاهُ عَنْ عَاتِقِهِ، انْكِحِي أُسَامَةَ». قَالَتْ: فَكَرِهْتُهُ، فَقَالَ: «انْكِحِي أُسَامَةَ» فَنَكَحْتُهُ فَجَعَلَ اللَّهُ فِيهِ خَيْرًا وَاغْتَبَطْتُ بِهِ

Transmission overview

Transmission path

  1. 01FatimaEarliest transmitterفَاطِمَةَ
  2. 02Abu Salama b. 'Abd al-Rahmanأَبِي سَلَمَةَ بْنِ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ
  3. 03'Abdullah b. Yazid, the client of al-Aswad b. Sufyanعَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ يَزِيدَ، مَوْلَى الْأَسْوَدِ بْنِ سُفْيَانَ
  4. 04Malikمَالِكٌ

Report842/ 174
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 187
English
Isnad

Ibrahim b. Sa'd b. Ibrahim b. 'Abd al-Rahman b. 'Awf informed us, from Ibn Shihab, from Salim, from his father ﵁, that the Messenger of God ﷺ said:

Matn

“When you see the new moon, fast; and when you see it, break the fast; and if it is obscured from you, estimate it.” And 'Abdullah used to fast the day before the new moon. Ibrahim b. Sa'd was asked: He would anticipate it? He said: Yes.

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا إِبْرَاهِيمُ بْنُ سَعْدِ بْنِ إِبْرَاهِيمَ بْنِ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنِ عَوْفٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، عَنْ سَالِمٍ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ رضي الله عنه، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ:

Matn

«إِذَا رَأَيْتُمُ الْهِلَالَ فَصُومُوا، وَإِذَا رَأَيْتُمُوهُ فَأَفْطِرُوا، فَإِنْ غُمَّ عَلَيْكُمْ فَاقْدُرُوا لَهُ» وَكَانَ عَبْدُ اللَّهِ يَصُومُ قَبْلَ الْهِلَالِ بِيَوْمٍ. قِيلَ لِإِبْرَاهِيمَ بْنِ سَعْدٍ: يَتَقَدَّمُهُ؟ قَالَ: نَعَمْ

Transmission overview

Report843/ 174
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 187
English
Isnad

Sufyan informed us, from 'Amr b. Dinar, from Muhammad b. Jubayr, from Ibn 'Abbas ﵄, who said:

Matn

I am amazed at one who anticipates the month, when the Messenger of God ﷺ said: “Do not fast until you see it, and do not break the fast until you see it.”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا سُفْيَانُ، عَنْ عَمْرِو بْنِ دِينَارٍ، عَنْ مُحَمَّدِ بْنِ جُبَيْرٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ عَبَّاسٍ رضي الله عنهما قَالَ:

Matn

عَجِبْتُ مِمَّنْ يَتَقَدَّمُ الشَّهْرَ وَقَدْ قَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم: «لَا تَصُومُوا حَتَّى تَرَوْهُ، وَلَا تُفْطِرُوا حَتَّى تَرَوْهُ»

  3. 03'Amr b. Dinarعَمْرِو بْنِ دِينَارٍ
  4. 04Sufyanسُفْيَانُ

Report844/ 174
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 187
English
Isnad

'Abd al-'Aziz b. Muhammad informed us, from Muhammad b. 'Amr, from Abu Salama, from Abu Hurayra ﵁, that the Messenger of God ﷺ said:

Matn

“Do not anticipate the month by a day or two, unless it coincides with a fast that one of you habitually fasts. Fast on seeing it and break the fast on seeing it; and if it is obscured from you, count thirty.”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا عَبْدُ الْعَزِيزِ بْنُ مُحَمَّدٍ، عَنْ مُحَمَّدِ بْنِ عَمْرٍو، عَنْ أَبِي سَلَمَةَ، عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ رضي الله عنه، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ:

Matn

«لَا تَقَدَّمُوا الشَّهْرَ بِيَوْمٍ وَلَا يَوْمَيْنِ إِلَّا أَنْ يُوَافِقَ ذَلِكَ صَوْمًا كَانَ يَصُومُهُ أَحَدُكُمْ، صُومُوا لِرُؤْيَتِهِ، وَأَفْطِرُوا لِرُؤْيَتِهِ، فَإِنْ غُمَّ عَلَيْكُمْ فَعُدُّوا ثَلَاثِينَ»

Report845/ 174
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 187
English
Isnad

'Amr b. Abi Salama informed us, from al-Awza'i: Yahya b. Abi Kathir narrated to me: Abu Salama narrated to me, from Abu Hurayra ﵁, who said: The Messenger of God ﷺ said:

Matn

“Do not anticipate Ramadan by a day or two, except a man who was (habitually) fasting a fast, let him fast it.”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا عَمْرُو بْنُ أَبِي سَلَمَةَ، عَنِ الْأَوْزَاعِيِّ، حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى بْنُ أَبِي كَثِيرٍ، حَدَّثَنِي أَبُو سَلَمَةَ، عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ رضي الله عنه قَالَ: قَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم:

Matn

«لَا تَقَدَّمُوا بَيْنَ يَدَيْ رَمَضَانَ بِيَوْمٍ وَلَا يَوْمَيْنِ، إِلَّا رَجُلًا كَانَ يَصُومُ صَوْمًا فَلْيَصُمْهُ»

Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 187
English
Isnad

Sufyan informed us, from Ibn Shihab, from Ibn al-Musayyib or Abu Salama, from Abu [p. 188] Hurayra—the doubt is from Sufyan—that the Messenger of God ﷺ said:

Matn

“The child belongs to the marriage bed, and for the adulterer is the stone.”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا سُفْيَانُ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ الْمُسَيِّبِ، أَوْ أَبِي سَلَمَةَ عَنْ أَبِي ⦗ص: 188⦘ هُرَيْرَةَ، الشَّكُّ مِنْ سُفْيَانَ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ:

Matn

«الْوَلَدُ لِلْفِرَاشِ، وَلِلْعَاهِرِ الْحَجَرُ»

Report847/ 174
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 188
English
Isnad

Sufyan b. 'Uyayna informed us, from al-Zuhri, from 'Urwa, from 'A'isha ﵂, that

Matn

'Abd b. Zam'a and Sa'd brought a dispute to the Messenger of God ﷺ about the son of a slave woman of Zam'a—he mentioned him. Sa'd said: O Messenger of God, my brother charged me, when I came to Mecca, to look for the son of Zam'a and take him, for he is my son. 'Abd b. Zam'a said: He is my brother and the son of my father's slave woman, born on my father's bed. He (the Prophet) saw a resemblance to 'Utba and said: “He is yours, O 'Abd b. Zam'a; the child belongs to the bed. And veil yourself from him, O Sawda.”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا سُفْيَانُ بْنُ عُيَيْنَةَ، عَنِ الزُّهْرِيِّ، عَنْ عُرْوَةَ، عَنْ عَائِشَةَ رضي الله عنها

Matn

أَنَّ عَبْدَ بْنَ زَمْعَةَ، وَسَعْدًا، اخْتَصَمَا إِلَى رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم فِي ابْنِ أَمَةِ زَمْعَةَ ذَكَرَهُ، فَقَالَ سَعْدٌ: يَا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ، أَوْصَانِي أَخِي إِذَا قَدِمْتُ مَكَّةَ أَنِ انْظُرْ إِلَى ابْنِ زَمْعَةَ فَاقْبِضْهُ فَإِنَّهُ ابْنِي، فَقَالَ عَبْدُ بْنُ زَمْعَةَ: أَخِي وَابْنُ أَمَةِ أَبِي، وُلِدَ عَلَى فِرَاشِ أَبِي، فَرَأَى شَبَهًا بِعُتْبَةَ فَقَالَ: «هُوَ لَكَ يَا عَبْدُ بْنُ زَمْعَةَ، الْوَلَدُ لِلْفِرَاشِ، وَاحْتَجِبِي مِنْهُ يَا سَوْدَةُ»

Transmission path

  2. 02'Urwaعُرْوَةَ

Isnad

Malik informed us, from Nafi', from Ibn 'Umar ﵄, that

Matn

the Messenger of God ﷺ separated the two who had exchanged li'an and attached the child to the woman.

Report849/ 174
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 188
English
Isnad

Sufyan b. 'Uyayna informed us, from 'Abdullah b. Abi Yazid, from his father, who said:

Matn

'Umar b. al-Khattab ﵁ sent for an old man of Banu Zuhra who lived in our house, and I went with him to 'Umar, who asked him about a birth from the births of the Jahiliyya. He said: As for the bed, it belongs to so-and-so, and as for the seed, to so-and-so. 'Umar, meaning Ibn al-Khattab ﵁, said: You have spoken truly, but the Messenger of God ﷺ ruled by the bed.

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا سُفْيَانُ بْنُ عُيَيْنَةَ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ أَبِي يَزِيدَ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ قَالَ:

Matn

أَرْسَلَ عُمَرُ بْنُ الْخَطَّابِ رضي الله عنه إِلَى شَيْخٍ مِنْ بَنِي زُهْرَةَ كَانَ يَسْكُنُ دَارَنَا، فَذَهَبْتُ مَعَهُ إِلَى عُمَرَ فَسَأَلَهُ عَنْ وِلَادٍ مِنْ وِلَادِ الْجَاهِلِيَّةِ فَقَالَ: أَمَّا الْفِرَاشُ فَلِفُلَانٍ، وَأَمَّا النُّطْفَةُ فَلِفُلَانٍ، فَقَالَ عُمَرُ يَعْنِي: ابْنَ الْخَطَّابِ رضي الله عنه: صَدَقْتَ، وَلَكِنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَضَى بِالْفِرَاشِ

Report850/ 174
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 188
English
Isnad

Ibrahim b. Sa'd informed us, from Ibn Shihab, from Sahl b. Sa'd, and he mentioned the hadith of the two who exchanged li'an, saying:

Matn

The Prophet ﷺ said: “Watch her; if she brings him forth dark, with intensely black eyes and large buttocks, then I think he has spoken the truth; and if she brings him forth ruddy, like a wahara (small lizard-like creature), then I think he is a liar.” And she brought him forth in the detestable description.

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا إِبْرَاهِيمُ بْنُ سَعْدٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، عَنْ سَهْلِ بْنِ سَعْدٍ، وَذَكَرَ، حَدِيثَ الْمُتَلَاعِنَيْنِ فَقَالَ:

Matn

قَالَ النَّبِيُّ صلى الله عليه وسلم: «أَبْصِرُوهَا، فَإِنْ جَاءَتْ بِهِ أَسْحَمَ أَدْعَجَ الْعَيْنَيْنِ، عَظِيمَ الْإِلْيَتَيْنِ فَلَا أُرَاهُ إِلَّا قَدْ صَدَقَ، وَإِنْ جَاءَتْ بِهِ أَحْمَرَ كَأَنَّهُ وَحَرَةٌ فَلَا أُرَاهُ إِلَّا كَاذِبًا». فَجَاءَتْ بِهِ عَلَى النَّعْتِ الْمَكْرُوهِ

  2. 02Ibn Shihabابْنِ شِهَابٍ
  3. 03Ibrahim b. Sa'dإِبْرَاهِيمُ بْنُ سَعْدٍ

Report851/ 174
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 188
English
Isnad

Ibrahim b. Sa'd informed us, from his father, from Sa'id ibn al-Musayyib and 'Ubaydullah b. 'Abd [p. 189] Allah, that the Prophet ﷺ said:

Matn

“If she brings forth a small reddish one with lank hair, he belongs to her husband; and if she brings him forth with little (black) eyes and curly hair, he belongs to the one whom he (the husband) accuses.” And she brought him forth with black eyes.

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا إِبْرَاهِيمُ بْنُ سَعْدٍ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، عَنْ سَعِيدِ بْنِ الْمُسَيِّبِ، وَعُبَيْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عَبْدِ ⦗ص: 189⦘ اللَّهِ، أَنَّ النَّبِيَّ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ:

Matn

«إِنْ جَاءَتْ أُمَيْغِرَ سَبِطًا فَهُوَ لِزَوْجِهَا، وَإِنْ جَاءَتْ بِهِ أُدَيْعِجَ جَعْدًا فَهُوَ لِلَّذِي يَتَّهِمُهُ». فَجَاءَتْ بِهِ أُدَيْعِجَ

Transmission path

Earliest to latest
  1. 01Sa'id ibn al-Musayyib and 'Ubaydullah b. 'Abd AllahEarliest transmitterسَعِيدِ بْنِ الْمُسَيِّبِ، وَعُبَيْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ
  2. 02his fatherأَبِيهِ
  3. 03Ibrahim b. Sa'dإِبْرَاهِيمُ بْنُ سَعْدٍ

Report852/ 174
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 189
English
Isnad

Sa'id ibn Salim informed us, from Ibn Abi Dhi'b, from Makhlad b. Khufaf, from 'Urwa, from 'A'isha ﵂, that

Matn

the Messenger of God ﷺ ruled that the yield goes with liability (al-kharaj bi'l-daman).

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا سَعِيدُ بْنُ سَالِمٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ أَبِي ذِئْبٍ، عَنْ مَخْلَدِ بْنِ خُفَافٍ، عَنْ عُرْوَةَ، عَنْ عَائِشَةَ رضي الله عنها

Matn

أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَضَى أَنَّ الْخَرَاجَ بِالضَّمَانِ

English
Isnad

Muslim b. Khalid informed us, from Hisham, from his father, from 'A'isha ﵂, that the Messenger of God ﷺ said:

Matn

“The yield goes with liability.”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا مُسْلِمُ بْنُ خَالِدٍ، عَنْ هِشَامٍ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، عَنْ عَائِشَةَ رضي الله عنها أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ:

Matn

«الْخَرَاجُ بِالضَّمَانِ»

Report854/ 174
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 189
English
Isnad

Malik informed us, from Abu al-Zinad, from al-A'raj, from Abu Hurayra ﵁, that the Prophet ﷺ said:

Matn

“Do not leave camels and sheep unmilked (to swell their udders); whoever buys them after that has the better of the two options after he has milked them: if he is satisfied with it he keeps it, and if he is displeased with it he returns it along with a sa' of dates.” Sufyan informed us, from Ayyub, from Ibn Sirin, from Abu Hurayra ﵁, from the Prophet ﷺ, the same, except that he said: “he returns it along with a sa' of dates, not wheat (samra').”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا مَالِكٌ، عَنْ أَبِي الزِّنَادِ، عَنِ الْأَعْرَجِ، عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ، رَضِيَ اللَّهُ تَعَالَى عَنْهُ أَنَّ النَّبِيَّ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ:

Matn

«لَا تُصَرُّوا الْإِبِلَ وَالْغَنَمَ، فَمَنِ ابْتَاعَهَا بَعْدَ ذَلِكَ فَهُوَ بِخَيْرِ النَّظَرَيْنِ بَعْدَ أَنْ يَحْلُبَهَا، إِنْ رَضِيَهَا أَمْسَكَهَا، وَإِنْ سَخِطَهَا رَدَّهَا وَصَاعًا مِنْ تَمْرٍ» أَخْبَرَنَا سُفْيَانُ، عَنْ أَيُّوبَ، عَنِ ابْنِ سِيرِينَ، عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ، رَضِيَ اللَّهُ تَعَالَى عَنْهُ عَنِ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم مِثْلَهُ إِلَّا أَنَّهُ قَالَ: «رَدَّهَا وَصَاعًا مِنْ تَمْرٍ، لَا سَمْرَاءَ»

Transmission path

Earliest to latest
  1. 01Abu HurayraEarliest transmitterأَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ
  2. 02al-A'rajالْأَعْرَجِ
  3. 03Abu al-Zinadأَبِي الزِّنَادِ
  4. 04Malikمَالِكٌ

English
Isnad

Malik informed us, from Nafi', from Ibn 'Umar ﵃, that the Messenger of God ﷺ said:

Matn

“Whoever buys food, let him not sell it until he has received it in full.”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا مَالِكٌ، عَنْ نَافِعٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ عُمَرَ رَضِيَ اللَّهُ تَعَالَى عَنْهُمْ أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ:

Matn

«مِنَ ابْتَاعَ طَعَامًا فَلَا يَبِعْهُ حَتَّى يَسْتَوْفِيَهُ»

Matn

English
Isnad

Sufyan informed us, from 'Amr b. Dinar, from Tawus, from Ibn 'Abbas ﵄, who said:

Matn

“What the Messenger of God ﷺ forbade is food being sold until it is received in full; and Ibn 'Abbas said, by his own opinion: I do not consider anything else to be but like it.”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا سُفْيَانُ، عَنْ عَمْرِو بْنِ دِينَارٍ، عَنْ طَاوُسٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ عَبَّاسٍ رضي الله عنهما قَالَ:

Matn

«أَمَّا الَّذِي نَهَى عَنْهُ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم فَهُوَ الطَّعَامُ أَنْ يُبَاعَ حَتَّى يُسْتَوْفَى وَقَالَ ابْنُ عَبَّاسٍ بِرَأْيِهِ، وَلَا أَحْسِبُ كُلَّ شَيْءٍ إِلَّا مِثْلَهُ»

Report858/ 174
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 189
English
Isnad

Sufyan informed us, from Ibn Abi Najih, from 'Abdullah b. Kathir, from Abu al-Minhal, from [p. 190] Ibn 'Abbas ﵄, who said:

Matn

The Prophet ﷺ came to Medina when they were paying in advance for dates for a year, two years and three; and the Messenger of God ﷺ said: “Whoever pays in advance, let him pay in advance for a known measure, a known weight, and a known term—or for a known term.”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا سُفْيَانُ، عَنِ ابْنِ أَبِي نَجِيحٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ كَثِيرٍ، عَنْ أَبِي الْمِنْهَالِ، عَنِ ⦗ص: 190⦘ ابْنِ عَبَّاسٍ رضي الله عنهما قَالَ:

Matn

قَدِمَ النَّبِيُّ صلى الله عليه وسلم الْمَدِينَةَ وَهُمْ يُسْلِفُونَ التَّمْرَ السَّنَةَ وَالسَّنَتَيْنِ وَالثَّلَاثَ، فَقَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم: «مَنْ أَسْلَفَ فَلْيُسْلِفْ فِي كَيْلٍ مَعْلُومٍ، وَوَزْنٍ مَعْلُومٍ، وَأَجَلٍ مَعْلُومٍ، أَوْ إِلَى أَجْلٍ مَعْلُومٍ»

Isnad

The trustworthy one informed us, from Ayyub, from Yusuf b. Mahak, from Hakim b. Hizam, who said:

Matn

The Messenger of God ﷺ forbade me to sell what I do not have.

Matn

Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 190
English
Isnad

Muslim informed us, from Ibn Abi Husayn, from 'Ata' and Tawus—I think he said:

Matn

and Mujahid and al-Hasan—that the Messenger of God ﷺ said on the day of the Conquest: “A believer is not to be killed for an unbeliever.”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا مُسْلِمٌ، عَنِ ابْنِ أَبِي حُسَيْنٍ، عَنْ عَطَاءٍ، وَطَاوُسٍ، أَحْسِبُهُ قَالَ:

Matn

وَمُجَاهِدٌ وَالْحَسَنُ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ يَوْمَ الْفَتْحِ: «وَلَا يُقْتَلُ مُؤْمِنٌ بِكَافِرٍ»

Report861/ 174
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 190
English
Isnad

Sufyan informed us, from Mutarrif, from al-Sha'bi, from Abu Juhayfa, who said:

Matn

I asked 'Ali: Do you have anything from the Messenger of God ﷺ besides the Qur'an? He said: No, by Him who split the seed and created the soul, except that God grants a servant understanding of His Book, and what is in the sheet. I said: And what is in the sheet? He said: The blood-money, the ransoming of the captive, and that a Muslim is not killed for an unbeliever. And in another place: and that a believer is not killed for an unbeliever.

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا سُفْيَانُ، عَنْ مُطَرِّفٍ، عَنِ الشَّعْبِيِّ، عَنْ أَبِي حُجَيْفَةَ قَالَ:

Matn

سَأَلْتُ عَلِيًّا: هَلْ عِنْدَكُمْ مِنْ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم شَيْءٌ سِوَى الْقُرْآنِ؟ فَقَالَ: لَا، وَالَّذِي فَلَقَ الْحَبَّةَ وَبَرَأَ النَّسَمَةَ، إِلَّا أَنْ يُعْطِيَ اللَّهُ عَبْدًا فَهْمًا فِي كِتَابِهِ، وَمَا فِي الصَّحِيفَةِ، فَقُلْتُ: وَمَا فِي الصَّحِيفَةِ؟ قَالَ: الْعَقْلُ، وَفِكَاكُ الْأَسِيرِ، وَلَا يُقْتَلُ مُسْلِمٌ بِكَافِرٍ. وَفِي مَوْضِعٍ آخَرَ: وَلَا يُقْتَلُ مُؤْمِنٌ بِكَافِرٍ

  2. 02al-Sha'biالشَّعْبِيِّ
  3. 03Mutarrifمُطَرِّفٍ
  4. 04Sufyanسُفْيَانُ

Isnad

Sufyan informed us, from al-Zuhri, from Haram b. Sa'd b. Muhayyisa, that

Matn

Muhayyisa asked the Prophet ﷺ about the earnings of the cupper, and he forbade it to him; but he kept speaking to him until he said: “Feed it to your slaves and feed it to your water-camel.”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا سُفْيَانُ، عَنِ الزُّهْرِيِّ، عَنْ حَرَامِ بْنِ سَعْدِ بْنِ مُحَيِّصَةَ،

Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 190
English
Isnad

Malik informed us, from al-Zuhri, from Haram b. Sa'd b. Muhayyisa, from his father, that

Matn

he asked the Prophet ﷺ for permission to hire out a cupper, and he forbade him that; but he kept asking him and seeking permission until he said: “Feed it to your water-camel and your slaves.”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا مَالِكٌ، عَنِ الزُّهْرِيِّ، عَنْ حَرَامِ بْنِ سَعْدِ بْنِ مُحَيِّصَةَ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ،

Matn

أَنَّهُ اسْتَأْذَنَ النَّبِيَّ صلى الله عليه وسلم فِي إِجَارَةِ الْحَجَّامِ فَنَهَاهُ عَنْهُ، فَلَمْ يَزَلْ يَسْأَلُهُ وَيَسْتَأْذِنُهُ حَتَّى قَالَ: «أَعْلِفْهُ نَاضِحَكَ وَرَقِيقَكَ»
